       nāttolaivu illā naṉṉūl pulavaṉum,
       āṭal pāṭal icaiyē tamiḻē 45
       paṇṇē pāṇi tūkkē muṭamē
       tēcikam eṉṟuivai āciṉ uṇarntu
       kūṭai nilattaik kuṟaivuiṉṟu mikuttuāṅku
       vāra nilattai vāṅkupu vāṅki
       vāṅkiya vārattu yāḻum kuḻalum 50
       ēṅkiya miṭaṟum icaivaṉa kēṭpak
       kūrukirk karaṇam kuṟiaṟintu cērtti
       ākkalum aṭakkalum mīttiṟam paṭāmaic
       cittirak karaṇam citaivuiṉṟi celuttum
       attaku taṇṇumai aruntoḻil mutalvaṉum, 55
       colliya iyalpiṉiṟ cittira vañcaṉai
       pulliya aṟintu puṇarppōṉ paṇpiṉ
       varttaṉai nāṉkum mayalaṟap peytuāṅku
       ēṟṟiya kuraliḷi eṉṟuiru narampiṉ
       oppak kēṭkum uṇarviṉaṉ ākip 60
       paṇamai muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟi aṟintu
       taṇṇumai mutalvaṉ taṉṉoṭu porunti
       vaṇṇap paṭṭaṭai yāḻmēl vaittuāṅku
       icaiyōṉ pāṭiya icaiyiṉ iyaṟkai
       vantatu vaḷarttu varuvatu oṟṟi 65
       iṉpuṟa iyakki icaipaṭa vaittu
       vāra nilattaik kēṭuiṉṟu vaḷarttuāṅku
       īra nilattiṉ eḻuttueḻuttu āka
       vaḻuviṉṟu icaikkum kuḻalōṉ tāṉum,
       īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟaik kēḷviyiṉ 70
       ōrēḻ pālai niṟuttal vēṇṭi
       vaṉmaiyiṟ kiṭanta tāra pākamum
       meṉmaiyiṟ kiṭanta kuraliṉ pākamum
       meykkiḷai narampiṟ kaikkiḷai koḷḷak
       kaikkiḷai oḻitta pākamum poṟpuṭait 75
       taḷarāt tāram viḷarikku īttuk
       kiḷaivaḻip paṭṭaṉaḷ, āṅkē kiḷaiyum
       taṉkiḷai aḻivukaṇṭu avaḷvayiṟ cēra
       ēṉai makaḷirum kiḷaivaḻic cēra
       mēlatu uḻaiyiḷi kīḻatu kaikkiḷai 80
       vampuuṟu marapiṉ cempālai āyatu
       iṟuti āti āka āṅkuavai
       peṟumuṟai vanta peṟṟiyiṉ nīṅkātu
       paṭumalai cevvaḻi pakararum pālaieṉak
       kuralkural ākat taṟkiḻamai tirintapiṉ 85
       muṉṉataṉ vakaiyē muṟaimaiyiṉ tirintuāṅku
       iḷimuta lākiya ērpaṭu kiḻamaiyum
       kōṭi viḷari mēṟcem pālaieṉa
       nīṭik kiṭanta kēḷvik kiṭakkaiyiṉ
       iṇainarampu uṭaiyaṉa aṇaivuṟak koṇṭuāṅku 90
       yāḻmēṟ pālai iṭamuṟai meliyak
       kuḻalmēṟ kōṭi valamuṟai meliya
       valivum melivum camaṉum ellām
       poliyak kōtta pulamai yōṉuṭaṉ,
       eṇṇiya nūlōr iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāatu 95
       maṇṇakam oruvaḻi vakuttaṉar koṇṭu
       puṇṇiya neṭuvaraip pōkiya neṭuṅkaḻaik
       kaṇṇiṭai orucāṇ vaḷarntatu koṇṭu
       nūlneṟi marapiṉ araṅkam aḷakkum
       kōlaḷavu irupattu nālviral āka 100
       eḻukōl akalattu eṇkōl nīḷattu
       orukōl uyarattu uṟuppiṉatu āki
       uttarap palakaiyoṭu araṅkiṉ palakai
       vaitta iṭainilam nāṟkōl āka
       ēṟṟa vāyil iraṇṭuṭaṉ poliyat 105
       tōṟṟiya araṅkil toḻutaṉar ēttap
       pūtarai eḻuti mēlnilai vaittut
       tūṇniḻal puṟappaṭa māṇviḷakku eṭuttuāṅku
       orumuka eḻiṉiyum porumuka eḻiṉiyum
       karantuvaral eḻiṉiyum purintuṭaṉ vakuttuāṅku 110
       ōviya vitāṉattu uraipeṟu nittilattu
       mālait tāmam vaḷaiyuṭaṉ nāṟṟi
       viruntupaṭak kiṭanta aruntoḻil araṅkattup
       pēricai maṉṉar peyarppuṟattu eṭutta
       cīriyal veṇkuṭaik kāmpunaṉi koṇṭu 115
       kaṇiṭai navamaṇi oḻukki maṇṇiya
       nāvalam polamtakaṭṭu iṭainilam pōkkik
       kāval veṇkuṭai maṉṉavaṉ kōyil
       intira ciṟuvaṉ cayantaṉ ākeṉa
       vantaṉai ceytu vaḻipaṭu talaikkōl 120
       puṇṇiya naṉṉīr poṟkuṭattu ēnti
       maṇṇiya piṉṉar mālai aṇintu
       nalamtaru nāḷāl polampūṇ ōṭai
       aracuuvāt taṭakkaiyil paraciṉar koṇṭu
       muracueḻuntu iyampap paliyam ārppa 125
       araicoṭu paṭṭa aimperuṅ kuḻuvum
       tērvalam ceytu kavikaik koṭuppa
       ūrvalam ceytu pukuntumuṉ vaittuāṅku,
       iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāa irukkai muṟaimaiyiṉ
       kuyiluva mākkaḷ neṟippaṭa niṟpa, 130
       valakkāl muṉmitittu ēṟi araṅkattu
       valattūṇ cērtal vaḻakkueṉap porunti
       inneṟi vakaiyāl iṭattūṇ cērnta
       tolneṟi iyaṟkait tōriya makaḷirum
       cīriyal poliya nīrala nīṅka 135
       vāram iraṇṭum varicaiyil pāṭap
       pāṭiya vārattu īṟṟilniṉṟu icaikkum
       kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ ellām
       kuḻalvaḻi niṉṟatu yāḻē, yāḻvaḻit
       taṇṇumai niṉṟatu takavē, taṇṇumaip 140
       piṉvaḻi niṉṟatu muḻavē, muḻavoṭu
       kūṭiniṉṟu icaittatu āman tirikai
       āman tirikaiyoṭu antaram iṉṟik
       koṭṭuiraṇṭu uṭaiyatuōr maṇṭilam ākak
       kaṭṭiya maṇṭilam patiṉoṉṟu pōkki 145
       vanta muṟaiyiṉ vaḻimuṟai vaḻāmal
       antarak koṭṭuṭaṉ aṭaṅkiya piṉṉar,
       mīttiṟam paṭāmai vakkāṇam vakuttup
       pāṟpaṭa niṉṟa pālaip paṇmēl
       nāṉkiṉ orīiya naṉkaṉam aṟintu 150
       mūṉṟuaḷantu oṉṟu koṭṭi ataṉai
       aintumaṇ ṭilattāl kūṭai pōkki
       vantavā ramvaḻi mayaṅkiya piṉṟai,
       āṟum nālum ammuṟai pōkkik
       kūṟiya aintiṉ koḷkai pōlap 155
       piṉṉaiyum ammuṟai pēriya piṉṟai,
       poṉiyal pūṅkoṭi purintuṭaṉ vakutteṉa
       nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl kaṭaip piṭittuk
       kāṭṭiṉaḷ ātaliṉ, kāval vēntaṉ
       ilaippūṅ kōtai iyalpiṉil vaḻāmait 160
       talaikkōl eytit talaiaraṅku ēṟi
       vitimuṟaik koḷkaiyiṉ āyirattu eṇkaḻañcu
       orumuṟai yākap peṟṟaṉaḷ atuvē
       nūṟupattu aṭukki eṭṭukkaṭai niṟutta
       vīṟuuyar pacumpoṉ peṟuvatuim mālai, 165
       mālai vāṅkunar cālumnam koṭikkueṉa
       māṉamar nōkkiōr kūṉikaik koṭuttu
       nakara nampiyar tiritaru maṟukil
       pakarvaṉar pōlvatōr pāṉmaiyiṉ niṟutta,
       māmalar neṭuṅkaṇ mātavi mālai 170
       kōvalaṉ vāṅkik kūṉi taṉṉoṭu
       maṇamaṉai pukku mātavi taṉṉoṭu
       aṇaivuṟu vaikaliṉ ayarntaṉaṉ mayaṅki
       viṭutal aṟiyā viruppiṉaṉ āyiṉaṉ.
       vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉtaṉ maṉaiyakam maṟantueṉ. 175
       (veṇpā)
       eṇṇum eḻuttum iyalaintum paṇnāṉkum
       paṇṇiṉṟa kūttup patiṉoṉṟum - maṇṇiṉmēl
       pōkkiṉāḷ pūmpukārp poṟṟoṭi mātavitaṉ
       vākkiṉāl āṭaraṅkil vantu.
       ------------------
                     ___________________________________

  nāvalar paṇṭita na.mu. vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭāravarkaḷ urai

   (kūttiyatu amaiti)
   1-7. teyva mālvarait tirumuṉi aruḷa
       eytiya cāpattu intira ciṟuvaṉoṭu
       talaikkōl tāṉattuc cāpam nīṅkiya
       malaippuaruñ ciṟappiṉ vāṉavar makaḷir
       ciṟappiṟ kuṉṟāc ceykaiyoṭu poruntiya
       piṟappiṟ kuṉṟāp peruntōḷ maṭantai
       tātuaviḻ purikuḻal mātavi taṉṉai

   teyva mālvarait tirumuṉi aruḷa-teyvat taṉmaiyuṭaiya periya malaiyākiya
   potiyiliṉkaṇ uḷḷa akattiya muṉivaṉ aruḷ ceytalāl, eytiya cāpattu intira
   ciṟuvaṉoṭu-muṉpu avaṉāl eytiya cāpattaiyuṭaiya intiraṉ kumāraṉākiya
   cayantaṉōṭum, talaikkōl tāṉattuc cāpam nīṅkiya-nāṭaka araṅkiṉkaṇṇē
   cāpam nīṅkap peṟṟa, malaipparuñ ciṟappiṉ vāṉavar makaḷir ciṟappiṟ
   kuṉṟāc ceykaiyoṭu poruntiya – māṟupaṭutalariya ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya
   arampaiyaril varicaiyiṟ kuṉṟāta nāṭakat toḻiloṭu poruntiya
   uruppaciyākiya am mātavi marapil vanta, piṟappiṟ kuṉṟāp peruntōḷ
   maṭantai-piṟappiṟ kuṉṟutalillāta periya tōḷaiyuṭaiya maṭantaiyākiya,
   tātu aviḻ purikuḻal mātavi taṉṉai-tātuviriyum pūkkaḷai yaṇinta kaṭai
   kuḻaṉṟu curuṇṭa kūntalaiyuṭaiya mātaviyai,
   muṉivaruṭ ciṟantōṉākaliṉ akattiyaṉait tirumuṉi eṉṟār.
   aruḷa-cāpamiṭṭaruḷa veṉṟumām. talaikkōṟṟāṉam-nāṭakavaraṅku. intira
   ciṟuvaṉoṭu cāpam nīṅkiya uruppaci yeṉka. ‘ceykaiyoṭu poruntiya’
   eṉpataṉpiṉ, uruppaciyākiya ammātavi marapil vanta eṉa viritturaikka.
   ‘cāpa nīṅkiya’ eṉpataṉpiṉ aṅṅaṉam viritturaittu vāṉavar makaḷir
   eṉpataṟkut taḷiyilār eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟuvar arumpatavurai yāciriyar.
   cayantaṉum, uruppaciyum cāpam peṟṟatum, atiṉīṅkiyatumākiya varalāṟu
   piṉṉark kaṭalāṭu kātaiyāṉum. aṭiyārkku nallār eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya paḻaiya
   mēṟkōḷāṉum viḷakkamuṟum; āṇṭuk kāṇka. aruḷāl eṉpatūum pāṭam.
   -------------
   8-9. āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum eṉṟuik
       kūṟiya mūṉṟiṉ oṉṟukuṟai paṭāmal

   āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum eṉṟu ikkūṟiya mūṉṟiṉ oṉṟu kuṟaipaṭāmal-kūttum
   pāṭṭum aḻakumeṉṟu collappaṭṭa im mūṉṟiṉuḷ oṉṟum kuṟaivupaṭāmal
   ivai,kuṟaivupaṭāmaikkuk,kāraṇamuṭaiyaḷākaliṉ eṉka.
   -------------
   10-11. ēḻāṇṭu iyaṟṟiōr īrāṟu āṇṭil
       cūḻkaṭal maṉṉaṟkuk kāṭṭal vēṇṭi,

   ēḻāṇṭu iyaṟṟi ōr īrāṟu āṇṭil cūḻkaḻal maṉṉaṟkuk kāṭṭal vēṇṭi-aiyāṇṭil
   taṇṭiyam piṭippittu ēḻāṇṭu iyaṟṟuvittup paṉṉīrāṇṭil vīrakkaḻal cūḻnta
   kāliṉaiyuṭaiya aracaṟku avaṉatu avaiyaraṅkēṟik kāṭṭalai virumpi,
   taṇṭiyam-kōl, maṉṉaṉ-cōḻaṉ karikārp
   peruvaḷattāṉ,eṉpararumpatavuraiyāciriyarum,aṭiyārkkunallārum. īṇṭuk
   karikālaṉ eṉṟu peyar kūṟappaṭāmaiyāṉum,
   "ceruveṅ kātaliṉ tirumarī vaḷavaṉ
       puṇṇiyat ticaimukam pōkiya vannāḷ"

   eṉa intira viḻavūreṭutta kātaiyiṉum,
   "maṉṉaṉ karikāl vaḷavaṉnīṅ kiyanāḷ
       innakar pōlvatōr iyalpiṉa tāki"

   eṉa maṇimēkalai yuḷḷum karikālaṉ vaṭaticaikkaṭ paṭaiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟamai
   kūṟappaṭṭiruttalaṉṟi, nikaḻkālattil vaittu avaṉ yāṇṭuṅ
   kūṟappaṭāmaiyāṉum, maturaikkāṇṭat tiṟutik kaṭṭuraiyil pāṇṭiyaṉ
   neṭuñceḻiyaṉaiyum vañcikkāṇṭat tiṟutik kaṭṭuraiyil cēran
   ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉaiyum kiḷantōtum aṭikaḷ pukārk kāṇṭat tiṟutik kaṭṭuraiyil
   cōḻaṉoruvaṉaiyum peyar kuṟittuk kūṟāmaiyāṉum karikālaṉ appoḻutiruntā
   ṉeṉṟu tuṇital cālāteṉka.
   paratacēṉāpatiyār kūṟiya, piṉvarum veṇpākkaḷ īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālaṉa.
   "paṇṇiyamvait tāṉaimukaṉ pātam paṇintunāṭ
       puṇṇiya vōrai pukaṉṟaṉakoṇ-ṭeṇṇiyē
       vaṇṭirukkuṅ kūntal maṭavaralai aiyāṇṭil
       taṇṭiyañcēr vippatē cālpu."
       "vaṭṭaṇaiyun tūciyu maṇṭalamum paṇṇamaiya
       eṭṭuṭa ṉīriraṇṭāṇ ṭeytiyapiṉ-kaṭṭaḷaiya
       kītak kuṟippum alaṅkāra muṅkiḷarac
       cōtit taraṅkēṟac cūḻ."
       "naṉṉar viruppuṭaiyōḷ naṟkuṇamu mikkuyarntōḷ
       coṉṉakulat tālamainta toṉmaiyaḷāyp – paṉṉiraṇṭāṇ
       ṭēyntataṟpiṉ āṭaluṭaṉ pāṭalaḻa kimmūṉṟum
       vāyntavaraṅ kēṟṟal vaḻakku."

   -----------
   (āṭalāciriyaṉ amaiti)
   12-25. iruvakaik kūttiṉ ilakkaṇam aṟintu
       palavakaik kūttum vilakkiṉiṟ puṇarttup
       patiṉōr āṭalum pāṭṭum koṭṭum
       vitimāṇ koḷkaiyiṉ viḷaṅka aṟintuāṅku
       āṭalum pāṭalum pāṇiyum tūkkum
       kūṭiya neṟiyiṉa koḷuttuṅ kālaip
       piṇṭiyum piṇaiyalum eḻiṟkaiyum toḻiṟkaiyum
       koṇṭa vakaiaṟintu kūttuvaru kālaik
       kūṭai ceytakai vārattuk kaḷaitalum
       vāram ceytakai kūṭaiyiṟ kaḷaitalum
       piṇṭi ceytakai āṭaliṟ kaḷaitalum
       āṭal ceytakai piṇṭiyiṟ kaḷaitalum
       kuravaiyum variyum viravala celutti
       āṭaṟku amainta ācāṉ taṉṉoṭum,

   iruvakaik kūttiṉ ilakkaṇam aṟintu-akak kūttum,puṟakkūttumākiya
   iruvakaik kūttiṉilakkaṇaṅkaḷaiyum aṟintu, palavakaik kūttum vilakkiṉiṟ
   puṇarntu-avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷākiya pala kūttukkaḷaiyum
   vilakkuṟuppukkaḷuṭaṉ puṇarkka vallaṉāy, patiṉōr āṭalum pāṭṭum
   koṭṭum-alliyam mutaṟ koṭukoṭṭi yīṟāykkiṭanta teyva
   viruttiyākiya,patiṉorukūttukkaḷaiyum akkūttukkaḷukkuriya pāṭṭukkaḷaiyum
   akkūttukkaḷiṉ vikaṟpaṅkaḷukkellām amainta vākkiyaṅkaḷiṉ kūṟukaḷaiyum,
   vitimāṇ koḷkaiyiṉ viḷaṅka aṟintu āṅku-vitittal māṭcimaip paṭṭa nūliṉ
   vaḻiyē viḷaṅkavaṟintu, āṭalum pāṭalum pāṇiyum tūkkum kūṭiya neṟiyiṉa
   koḷuttuṅkālai-āṭalum pāṭalum tāḷaṅkaḷum tāḷaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻivarum
   tūkkukkaḷum tammiṟ kūṭiya neṟiyiṉavāka nikaḻttumiṭattu, piṇṭiyum
   piṇaiyalum eḻiṟkaiyum toḻiṟkaiyum koṇṭavakaiyaṟintu – piṇṭi piṇaiyal
   eḻiṟkai toḻiṟkai eṉṟu collappaṭṭa nāṉkiṉaiyum koḷḷutaṟkuriya vakaiyiṉai
   aṟintu, kūttu varukālai-iruvakaik kūttukkaḷum nikaḻumiṭattu, kūṭai
   ceyta kai vārattuk kaḷaitalum, vāram ceyta kai kūṭaiyiṟ
   kaḷaitalum-kūṭaik katiyākac ceyta kai vārak katiyuṭ pukāmalum,
   vārakkatiyākac ceyta kai kūṭaikkatiyuṭ pukāmalum kaḷaitalum, piṇṭi
   ceyta kaiāṭaliṟ kaḷaitalum, āṭal ceyta kai piṇṭiyiṟ kaḷaitalum-āṭal
   nikaḻuṅkāl avinayam nikaḻāmalum, avinayam nikaḻuṅkāl āṭal nikaḻāmalum
   kaḷaitalum pēṇi, kuravaiyum variyum viravala celutti-kuravaik kūttum
   varik kūttum tammil viravātapaṭi celutti, āṭaṟku amainta ācāṉ
   taṉṉoṭum-ivvāṟu āṭavum āṭṭuvikkavum valla āṭalāciriyaṉōṭum;
   arumpatavuraiyāciriyarum aṭiyārkku nallārum iruvakaik kūttu eṉpataṟku
   iruvakaippaṭṭa akakkūttu eṉṟum, palavakaikkūttu eṉpataṟkup
   palavakaippaṭṭa puṟa naṭaṅkaḷ eṉṟum poruḷ kūṟiṉar. avaruḷ muṉṉavar,
   "ivvakaik kūttāvaṉa: tēci, mārkkam eṉa vivai; "mārkkameṉpatu vaṭukiṉ
   peyarē" eṉṟum, piṉṉavar: "iruvakaik kūttāvaṉa; vacaikkūttu,
   pukaḻkkūttu; vēttiyal, potuviyal; varikkūttu, variccāntik kūttu; cāntik
   kūttu, vinōtakkūttu; āriyam, tamiḻ; iyalpukkūttu, tēcikkūttu eṉap
   palavakaiya" eṉṟum, "īṇṭu iruvakaik kūttāvaṉa: cāntiyum, vinōtamum"
   eṉṟum viḷakkam kūṟiṉar. akakkūttellām tēci, mārkkam eṉa aṭaṅkāmaiyālum,
   vacai, vari, vinōtam mutaliyaṉa akakkūttuḷ aṭaṅkāmaiyāṉum, iruvakaik
   kūttu eṉpataṟku akamum puṟamumākiya iruvakaik kūttu eṉṟalē
   poruttamākum. iraṇṭiraṇṭāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavai yellām vevvēṟiyalpu
   paṟṟi vēṟu vēṟu peyarāṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭuvaṉa vaṉṟi, vevvēṟu kūttukkaḷalla.
   ivvāṟṟāl avai yellāvaṟṟaiyum, akam, puṟam eṉṟātal, vēttiyal potuviyal
   eṉṟātal, cānti, vinōtam eṉṟātal pākupaṭuttal poruntum.
   vēttiyal – aracarkkāṭuvatu. potuviyal-ēṉaiyōrkkāṭuvatu. vēttiyalai
   akameṉṟum, potuviyalaip puṟameṉṟum kūṟuvārumuḷar.
   "cāntik kūttē talaiva ṉiṉpam
       ēntiniṉ ṟāṭiya vīriru naṭamavai
       cokka meyyē yavinaya nāṭakam
       eṉṟip pāṟpaṭuu meṉmaṉār pulavar"

   eṉpataṉāl, cāntikkūttu nālvakaippaṭum; avaṟṟuḷ, cokkam eṉpatu cutta
   niruttam; āvatu tāḷa layattai ātāramāka vuṭaiyatu. meykkūttu eṉpatu
   meyttoḻiṟ kūttu eṉṟum, atu tēci, vaṭuku ciṅkaḷam eṉa mūvakaippaṭum
   eṉṟum kūṟuvar. iḵtu uḷḷak kuṟippai ātāramākak koṇṭatu pōlum. avinayak
   kūttu eṉpatu katai taḻuvātu pāṭṭiṉ poruḷukkuk kai kāṭṭi vallapañ ceyyuṅ
   kūttu. nāṭakam eṉpatu katai taḻuvi varuṅkūttu. innāṉkiṉum iṟutik kaṇ
   niṉṟa nāṭakam ciṟantatātal ōrntuṇarka. ivaiyāvum nāyakaṉ cāntamāka
   āṭiya kūttātaliṉ cāntik kūtteṉappaṭum eṉpar.
   vinōtak kūttu eṉpatil kuravai, kalinaṭam, kuṭakkūttu, karaṇam,
   nōkku,tōṟpāvai eṉpaṉa aṭaṅkumeṉpar. avaṟṟuḷ,
   kuravai eṉpatu kāmamum veṉṟiyum roruḷākak kuravaic ceyyuḷ pāṭṭāka
   eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉmarēṉum kaipiṇaintāṭuvatu.
   kalinaṭam eṉpatu kaḻāykkūttu.
   kuṭakkūt teṉpatu patiṉōrāṭaluḷ oṉṟu
   karaṇamāvatu paṭintavāṭal.
   nōkkeṉpatu pāramum nuṇmaiyum māyamum mutalāyiṉavaṟṟai yuṭaiyatu.
   tōṟpāvai yeṉpatu tōlāṟpāvai ceytu āṭṭuvippatu.
   iṉṉum nakaittiṟac cuvaiyuṭaiya vitūṭakak kūttiṉōṭu ēḻeṉpārum, veṟiyāṭṭu
   mutalākat teyvamēṟi yāṭuṅ kūttiṉaik kūṭṭi ēḻeṉpāru muḷar. vitūṭakak
   kūttu vacaikkūttu; atu vēttiyal, potuviyal eṉa iraṇṭu vakaippaṭumeṉpar.
   1u. iruvakaik kūttiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa: "aṟuvakai nilaiyum aivakaip
   pātamum īreṇ vakaiya aṅkak kiriyaiyum, varuttaṉai nāṉkum niruttakkai
   muppatum, attaku toḻila vākumeṉpa" eṉṟōtappaṭṭaṉa.
   13. palavakaik kūttum eṉṟatu mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa akakkūttu mutaliyavaṟṟuṭaṉ
   veṉṟik kūttum, vacaikkūttu mākum. "palvakai yeṉpatu pakaruṅkālai, veṉṟi
   vacaiyō vinōta mākum."eṉpatoru cūttiraṅ kāṭṭuvar; avaṟṟuḷ "māṟṟā
   ṉoṭukkamum maṉṉa ṉuyarcciyum, mēṟpaṭakkūṟum veṉṟik kūttē" eṉavum,
   "palvakai yuruvamum paḻittuk kāṭṭa, valla ṉātal vacaiyeṉap paṭumē"
   eṉavum veṉṟi, vacaikkūttukaṭku ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvar.
   vilakkiṉiṉ – vilakkuṟuppōṭu: vēṟṟumai mayakkam. vilakkuṟuppu eṉṟa
   coṟkup poruḷ vēntu vilakku, paṭaivilakku, ūrvilakku eṉṉum
   vilakkukaḷākiya pāṭṭukkaḷukku uṟuppāyi varuvatu eṉṟum, talaivaṉ
   celuttukiṉṟa kataiyai vilakkiyum akkataiyai naṭāttiyum muṉpu ceyta
   kataikkē uṟuppākuvatu eṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar;
   "vilakkuṟup peṉpatu virikkuṅ kālaip
       poruḷum yōṉiyum viruttiyuñ cantiyum
       cuvaiyuñ cātiyuṅ kuṟippuñ cattuvamum
       avinayañ collē colvakai vaṇṇamum
       variyuñ cētamum uḷappaṭat tokaii
       icaiya veṇṇi ṉīrē ḻuṟuppē"

   eṉṉuñ cūttirattāl, vilakkuṟuppukkaḷ patiṉāṉku vakaiyiṉavātal
   peṟappaṭum.
   ippatiṉāṉkaṉuḷ poruḷ, cāti, yōṉi, virutti eṉṉum nāṉkum oruvakaiyum;
   cuvai, kuṟippu, cattuvam, avinayam eṉṉum nāṉkum oruvakaiyum; col,
   colvakai, vaṇṇam, vari eṉṉum nāṉkum oruvakaiyum; canti, cētam eṉṉum
   iraṇṭum oruvakaiyumākap pākupaṭum.
   avaṟṟuḷ, poruḷ nāṉku vakaippaṭum; avai: aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu
   eṉpaṉa. ivai nāṭakattiṟ kūṭiyum kuṟaintum varumiṭattu nāṭakam,
   pirakaraṇap pirakaraṇam, pirakaraṇam, aṅkam eṉap peyar vēṟupaṭum.
   aṟamutal nāṉkum amaintatu mutalatum, aṟam poruḷ iṉpam amaintatu
   iramṭāvatum, aṟam poruḷ amaintatu mūṉṟāvatum, aṟamoṉṟum amaintatu
   nāṉkāvatum ām; ivai nāṉkum nāṭakamē.
   ivaitām muṟaiyē antaṇar mutaliya cātikaḷākavum kūṟappaṭum.
   yōṉiyāvatu poruḷ tōṉṟumiṭam uḷḷōṉ talaivaṉāka uḷḷatoru poruṇ
   mēṟceytalum, illōṉ talaivaṉāka uḷḷatoru poruṇmēṟ ceytalum, uḷḷōṉ
   talaivaṉāka illatoru poruṇmēṟ ceytalum, illōṉ talaivaṉāka illatoru
   poruṇmēṟ ceytalum eṉa atu nāṉku vakaippaṭum. eṉṉai?
   "uḷḷōṟ kuḷḷatum illōṟ kuḷḷatum
       uḷḷōṟ killatum illōṟ killatum
       eḷḷā turaittal yōṉi yākum."

   eṉṟārākaliṉ.
   viruttiyāvatu nāṭakattiṉ iyalpu allatu taṉmai. cāttuvati, ārapaṭi,
   kaiciki, pārati eṉa virutti nālvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, cāttuvati yeṉpatu
   aṟam poruḷākat teyvamāṉiṭar talaivarāka varuvatu. ārapaṭi yeṉpatu poruḷ
   poruḷāka vīrarākiya māṉiṭar talaivarāka varuvatu. kaiciki yeṉpatu kāmam
   poruḷākak kāmukarākiya makkaḷ talaivarāka varuvatu. pāratiyāvatu kūttaṉ
   talaivaṉāka naṭaṉ naṭi poruḷākak kāṭṭiyum uraittum varuvatu. cuvai
   oṉpatu vakaippaṭum: avai: vīram, accam, iḻippu, viyappu, kāmam, avalam,
   vekuḷi, nakai, camanilai eṉpaṉa. vēmpeṉṉum poruḷum nāveṉṉum poṟiyum
   kūṭiyavaḻi nāvāl uṇarappaṭum kaippuccuvai pōlac cuvaikkappaṭum poruḷum
   ataṉaic cuvaikkum poṟiyum kūṭiyavaḻippiṟakkum poṟiyuṇarvu
   cuvaiyeṉappaṭum.
   kuṟippāvatu: accuvai yuṇarvu maṉattuṭ paṭṭavaḻi uḷḷattē nikaḻuṅ
   kuṟippākum.
   cattuvamāvatu: uḷḷattu nikaḻum kuṟippiṉukkēṟpa uṭampiṉkaṇ nikaḻum
   vēṟupāṭu. itu viṟal eṉavum paṭum. meymmayir cilirttal, kaṇṇīr vārtal,
   naṭukkamaṭuttal, viyarttal, tēṟṟam, kaḷittal, viḻittal, vetumpal,
   cākkāṭu, kuraṟcitaivu eṉac cattuvam pattu vakaippaṭum eṉpar. vaṭanūlār
   cattuvattai eṇvakaippaṭuttuc ciṟitu vēṟupaṭa vuraippar. cuvaipporuḷ,
   poṟiyuṇarvu,kuṟippu, viṟal eṉṉum nāṉkuṅ kūṭiyapoḻutu
   cuvaiyeṉṉum meyppāṭu tōṉṟum.
   oṉpāṉ cuvaikaṭkuriya viṟalkaḷ avvac cuvai yavinayaṅkaḷ eṉavum paṭum.
   avaṟṟaip piṉvaruñ cūttiraṅkaḷāl aṟika:
   " vīraccuvai yavinayam viḷampuṅ kālai, murinta puruvamuñ civanta
   kaṇṇum, piṭitta vāḷuṅ kaṭitta veyiṟum, maṭitta vutaṭuñ curuṭṭiya
   nutalum, tiṇṇeṉa vuṟṟa collum pakaivarai, eṇṇal cellā vikaḻcciyum,
   piṟavum, naṇṇu meṉpa naṉkuṇarntōrē."
   "acca vavinayam āyuṅ kālai, oṭuṅkiya vuṭampum naṭuṅkiya nilaiyum,
   malaṅkiya kaṇṇuṅ kalaṅkiya vuḷaṉum, karantuvara luṭaimaiyuṅ kaiyetir
   maṟuttalum, paranta nōkkamu micaipaṇ piṉavē."
   "iḻippi ṉavinayam iyampum kālai, iṭuṅkiya kaṇṇu meyiṟu puṟampōtalum,
   oṭuṅkiya mukamu muñaṟṟāk kālum cōrnta yākkaiyuñ coṉṉiram pāmaiyum,
   nērntaṉa veṉpa neṟiyaṟintōrē."
   "aṟputa avinayam aṟivarak kiḷappiṉ, coṟcōr vuṭaiyatu cōrnta kaiyatu,
   meymmayir kuḷirppatu viyattaka vuṭaiyatu, eytiya timaittalum viḻittalu
   mikavāteṉ, ṟaiyamil pulava raṟaintaṉareṉpa"
   "kāma vavinayaṅ karutuṅ kālait tūvuḷ ḷuṟutta vaṭivun toḻilum, kārikai
   kalanta kaṭaikkaṇuṅ, kaviṉpeṟu mūral muṟuval ciṟu nilā varumpalum,
   malarnta mukaṉu mirantameṉ kiḷaviyum, kalantaṉa piṟavuṅ kaṭaippiṭit
   taṉarē."
   "avalat tavinayam aṟivarak kiḷappiṉ kavalaiyoṭu puṇarnta kaṇṇīr
   māriyum, vāṭiya nīrmaiyum varuntiya celavum, pīṭaḻi yiṭumpaiyum
   pitaṟṟiya collum niṟaikai yaḻitalum nīrmaiyil kiḷaviyum, poṟaiyiṉ
   mukalum puṇarttiṉar pulavar."
   "vekuḷiccuvai yavinayattai uṇarttuñ cūttiram citaintu viṭṭatu. "
   kaipicaiyā vāymaṭiyāk kaṇcivavā veytuyirā, meykulaiyā vērā
   vekuṇṭeḻuntāṉ"eṉṉum taṇṭiyalaṅkāra mēṟkōḷāl ataṉaiyaṟika.
   "nakaiyi ṉavinayam nāṭṭuṅ kālai, mikaipaṭu nakaiyatu
   piṟarnakaiyuṭaiyatu, kōṭṭiya mukattatu..... viṭṭumuri puruvamoṭu
   vilāvuṟuppuṭaiyatu, ceyvatu piṟitāy vēṟucē tippateṉ, ṟaiyamil
   pulavaraṟaintaṉa reṉpa."
   "nāṭṭuṅ kālai, naṭuvunilai yavinayam, kōṭpā ṭaṟiyāk koḷ kaiyu māṭciyum,
   aṟantaru neñcamum āṟiya viḻiyum, piṟaḻnta kāṭci nīṅkiya nilaiyum,
   kuṟippiṉ ṟākalun tuṇukka millāt takaimika vuṭaimaiyun taṇṇeṉa
   vuṭaimaiyum, aḷattaṟ karumaiyu maṉpoṭu puṇartalum, kalakkamoṭu puṇarnta
   nōkkuṅ katirppum, vilakkāreṉpa vēṇṭu moḻippulavar."
   avinayam eṉpatu pāvakam. muṟkuṟitta cuvainilai yavinayaṅkaḷaṉṟi, vēṟu
   irupattu nāṉku avinayaṅkaḷ uḷa. avai: vekuṇṭōṉ avinayam. aiyamuṟṟōṉ
   avinayam, cōmpiṉōṉ avinayam, kaḷittōṉ avinayam, uvantōṉ avinayam,
   aḻukkāṟuṭaiyōṉ avinayam, iṉpamuṟṟōṉ avinayam, teyvamuṟṟōṉ avinayam,
   ñaññaiyuṟṟōṉ avinayam, uṭaṉpaṭṭōṉ avinayam, uṟaṅkiṉōṉ avinayam,
   tuyiluṇarntōṉ avinayam, cettōṉ avinayam, maḻai peyyappaṭṭōṉ avinayam,
   paṉittalaippaṭṭōṉ avinayam, veyiṟṟalaippaṭṭōṉ avinayam, nāṇamuṟṟōṉ
   avinayam, varuttamuṟṟōṉ avinayam kaṇṇōvuṟṟōṉ avinayam, talainōvuṟṟōṉ
   avinayam, aḻaṟṟiṟampaṭṭōṉ avinayam, cītamuṟṟōṉ avinayam, veppamuṟṟōṉ
   avinayam, nañcuṇṭōṉ avinayam eṉpaṉa. ivaiyaṉaittiṟkum aṭiyārkku nallār
   kāṭṭiya nūṟpākkaḷ varumāṟu:
   "vekuṇṭōṉ avinayam viḷampuṅ kālai, maṭitta vāyu malarnta mārpun,
   tuṭitta puruvamuñ cuṭṭiya viralum, kaṉṟiṉa uḷḷamoṭu kaiyuṭait
   tiṭutalum, aṉṉa nōkkamō ṭāyntaṉar koḷalē."
   "poyyil kāṭcip pulavōr āynta, aiya muṟṟōṉ avinayam uraippiṉ, vāṭiya
   uṟuppum mayaṅkiya nōkkamum, pīṭaḻi pulaṉum pēcā tiruttalum, piṟaḻnta
   ceykaiyum vāṉṟicai nōkkalum, aṟaintaṉar piṟavum aṟinticiṉōrē."
   "maṭiyiṉ avinayam vakukkuṅ kālai, noṭiyoṭu palakoṭṭāvi mika
   vuṭaimaiyum, mūri nimirttalum muṉivoṭu puṇarttaluṅ, kāraṇamiṉṟi yāḻntu
   maṭin tiruttalum, piṇiyu miṉṟic cōrnta celavō, ṭaṇitaru pulavar āyntaṉa
   reṉpa."
   "kaḷittōṉ avinayam kaḻaṟuṅ kālai, oḷittalai oḷiyāṉ uraitta liṉmaiyum,
   kaviḻntuñ cōrntun tāḻntut talarntum, vīḻnta colloṭu miḻaṟṟic cāytalum,
   kaḷikaik kavarnta kaṭaikkaṇōk kuṭaimaiyum, pēricai yāḷar pēṇiṉar
   koḷalē."
   "uvantōṉ avinayam uraikkuṅ kālai, nivantiṉi tākiya kaṇmala ruṭaimaiyum,
   iṉiti ṉiyaṉṟa uḷḷa muṭaimaiyum, muṉiviṉakaṉṟa muṟuvaṉakai yuṭaimaiyum,
   irukkaiyuñ cēṟaluṅ kāṉamum piṟavum, oruṅkuṭa ṉamainta kuṟippiṟ ṟaṉṟē."
   "aḻukkā ṟuṭaiyōṉ avinayam uraippiṉ, iḻukkoṭu puṇarnta
   icaipporuḷuṭaimaiyuṅ, kūmpiya vāyuṅ kōṭiya uraiyum, ōmpātu vitirkkum
   kaivakai yuṭaimaiyum, āraṇaṅ kākiya vekuḷi uṭaimaiyuṅ, kāraṇa miṉṟi
   melintamuka muṭaimaiyu, melivoṭu puṇarnta iṭumpaiyu mēvarap poliyu
   meṉpa poruntu moḻippulavar";
   "iṉpamoṭu puṇarntōṉ avinayam iyampiṉ, tuṉpam nīṅkit tuvartta yākkaiyun,
   tayaṅkit tāḻnta perumakiḻ vuṭaimaiyum, mayaṅki vanta celavunaṉi
   yuṭaimaiyum, aḻakuḷ ḷuṟutta coṟpoli vuṭaimaiyum eḻiloṭu puṇarnta
   narumalaruṭaimaiyum kalaṅkaḷ cērntakaṉṟa tōṇmār puṭaimaiyum, nalaṅkeḻu
   pulavar nāṭiṉa reṉpa";
   "teyvamuṟṟōṉ avinayam ceppiṟ, kaiviṭṭeṟinta kalakka muṭaimaiyum,
   maṭitteyiṟu kauviya vāyttoḻi luṭaimaiyum, tuṭitta puruvamun tuḷaṅkiya
   nilaiyuñ, ceyya mukamuñ cērnta cerukkum, eytu meṉpa iyalpuṇarntōrē";
   "ñaññai yuṟṟō ṉavinayam nāṭil, paṉmeṉ ṟiṟukiya nāvaḻi vuṭaimaiyum,
   nuraicērntu kūmpum vāyum nōkkiṉark, kuraippōṉ pōla uṇarvi lāmaiyum,
   viḻippōṉ pōla viḻiyā tiruttalum, viḻuttaka vuṭaimaiyum oḻukki lāmaiyum,
   vayaṅkiya tirumukam aḻuṅkalum piṟavum, mēviyateṉpa viḷaṅkumoḻip
   pulavar";
   "cintaiyuṭam paṭṭōṉ avinayam teriyiṉ, muntai yāyiṉum uṇarā nilaimaiyum,
   piṭitta kaimēl aṭainta kaviṉum, muṭittaluṟāta karuma nilaimaiyuñ,
   colvatu yātum uṇarā nilaimaiyum, pullu meṉpa poruntumoḻippulavar";
   "tuñcā niṉṟōṉ avinayam tuṇiyiṉ, eñcāta liṉṟi irupuṭai maruṅku,
   malarntuṅ kaviḻntum varupaṭai yiyaṟṟiyu malarntuyirp puṭaiya
   āṟṟalumākum.";
   "iṉṉuyi luṇarntōṉ avinayam iyampiṉ, oṉṟiya kuṟuṅ koṭṭāviyum uyirppun
   tūṅkiya mukamun tuḷaṅkiya uṭampum, ōṅkiya tiripum oḻintavuṅ koḷalē.";
   "cettōṉ avinayam ceppuṅ kālai, attaka accamum aḻippum ākkalum, kaṭitta
   niraippaliṉ veṭittup poṭittup, pōnta tuṇi vuṭaimaiyum
   valitta uṟuppum, melinta vakaṭu meṉmaimika vuṭaimaiyum, veṇmaṇi tōṉṟak
   karumaṇi karattalum, uṇmaiyiṟ pulavar uṇarnta vāṟē";
   " maḻaipeyyap paṭṭōṉ avinayam vakukkiṉ, iḻitaka vuṭaiya tiyalpunaṉi
   yuṭaimaiyum,meykūr naṭukkamum piṇittalum paṭāttai, meypūṇ ṭoṭukkiya
   mukattoṭu puṇarttalum, oḷippaṭu maṉaṉilulaṟiya kaṇṇum, viḷiyiṉun
   tuḷiyiṉu maṭintacevi yuṭaimaiyuṅ koṭuki viṭṭeṟinta kuḷirmika
   vuṭaimaiyum, naṭuṅku palloli yuṭaimaiyu muṭiyak, kaṉavukaṇ ṭāṟṟā
   ṉeḻutalu muṇṭē";
   "paṉittalaip paṭṭōṉ avinayam pakariṉ, naṭukka muṭaimaiyum nakaipaṭu
   nilaimaiyuñ, coṟṟaḷarn ticaittalu maṟṟami lavatiyum, pōrvai viḻaitalum
   puntinō vuṭaimaiyum, nīṟām viḻiyuñ cēṟu muṉitalum, iṉṉavai piṟavum
   icaittaṉar koḷalē";
   "uccip poḻutiṉ vantōṉ avinayam, ecca miṉṟi iyampuṅ kālaic coriyā niṉṟa
   peruntuya ruḻantu, teriyā niṉṟa uṭamperi yeṉṉac civanta kaṇṇum ayarnta
   nōkkamum, payanta teṉpa paṇpuṇarntōrē";
   "nāṇa muṟṟōṉ avinayam nāṭiṉ, iṟaiñciya talaiyum maṟainta ceykaiyum,
   vāṭiya mukamum kōṭiya uṭampum, keṭṭa voḷiyum kīḻkkaṇ ṇōkkamum, oṭṭiṉa
   reṉpa uṇarntici ṉōrē"; "varutta muṟṟōṉ avinayam vakuppiṟ, porutta
   millāp puṉka ṇuṭaimaiyuñ, cōrnta yākkaiyuñ
   cōrnta muṭiyuṅ, kūrnta viyarvuṅ kuṟumpal luyāvum, vaṟṟiya vāyum
   vaṇaṅkiya uṟuppum uṟṟateṉpa uṇarntici ṉōrē";
   "kaṇṇō vuṟṟōṉ avinayam kāṭṭi, ṉaṇṇiya kaṇṇīrt tuḷiviral teṟittalum,
   vaḷaintapuru vattoṭu vāṭiya mukamum, veḷḷiṭai nōkkiṉ viḻitaru maccamun,
   teḷḷitiṟ pulavar teḷintaṉar koḷalē";
   "talainō vuṟṟōṉ avinayam cāṟṟiṉ, nilaimai yiṉṟittalaiyāṭṭuṭaimaiyuṅ,
   kōṭiya virukkaiyun taḷarnta vēroṭu, peruviraliṭukkiya nutalum varunti,
   oṭuṅkiya kaṇṇoṭu piṟavun, tiruntumeṉpa cenneṟip pulavar";
   "aḻaṟṟiṟam paṭṭōṉ avinayam uraippiṉ, niḻaṟṟiṟam vēṇṭum neṟimaiyiṉ
   viruppum, aḻalum veyiluñ,cuṭarum añcalum, niḻalum nīruñ cēṟu
   muvattalum, paṉinī ruvappum pātirit toṭaiyalum, nuṉivira līra maruneṟi
   yākkalum, pukka tuṉpōṭu pularnta yākkaiyum, tokka teṉpa tuṇivaṟin
   tōrē";
   "cīta muṟṟōṉ avinayam ceppiṉ, otiya paruvara luḷḷamō ṭuḻattalu, mīra
   mākiya pōrvai yuṟuttalu, māra veyiluḻan taḻalum vēṇṭalu muraciyu
   muraṉṟu muyirttu muraittalun, takkaṉa piṟavuñ cāṟṟiṉar pulavar";
   "veppiṉ avinayam virikkuṅ kālait tappil kaṭaippiṭit taṉmaiyun tākamum,
   eriyi ṉaṉṉa vemmaiyō ṭiyaivum, veruvaru miyakkamum vempiya viḻiyum
   nīruṇ vēṭkaiyu nirampā valiyum, ōruṅ kālai uṇarntaṉar koḷalē";
   "koñciya moḻiyiṟ kūreyiṟu maṭittalum, pañciyiṉ vāyiṟ paṉinurai
   kūmpalun, tañca māntar tammuka nōkkiyōr, iṉcoliyampuvāṉ pōliyam
   pāmaiyum, nañcuṇ ṭōṉṟaṉ avinayam eṉpa";
   " colliya vaṉṟiyum varuvaṉa vuḷaveṉiṟ, pulluvaḻic cērttip poruntuvaḻip
   puṇarppa;"
   iṉi, col eṉṉum uṟuppu mūṉṟu vakaippaṭum. avai , uṭcol, puṟaccol, ākāyac
   col eṉpaṉa. neñcoṭu kūṟal uṭcollum, kēṭpōrkkuraittal puṟaccollum, tāṉē
   kūṟal ākāyaccollum ām.
   colvakai nāṉku vakaippaṭum. avai : cuṇṇam, curitakam, vaṇṇam, varitakam
   eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, cuṇṇam nāṉkaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; curitakam eṭṭaṭiyāṉ
   varuvatu; vaṇṇam patiṉāṟaṭiyāṉ varuvatu; varitakam muppattiraṇṭaṭiyāṉ
   varuvatu.
   vaṇṇamāṉatu oru vakaiyāṉ peruvaṇṇam, iṭaivaṇṇam, vaṉappuvaṇṇameṉa mūṉṟu
   vakaippaṭumeṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ, peruvaṇṇam āṟāyum, iṭaivaṇṇam
   irupattoṉṟāyum, vaṉappuvaṇṇam nāṟpattoṉṟāyum varumeṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
   āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār irupatu vaṇṇaṅ kūṟiṉar. nūṟuvaṇṇaṅ
   kūṟiṉārumuḷar.
   variyāvatu : varikkūttukkuriya pāṭal.
   "varippāṭalāvatu : paṇṇum, tiṟamum, ceyalum, pāṇiyum oru neṟiyaṉṟi
   mayaṅkac collappaṭṭa eṭṭaṉiyalpum, āṟaṉiyalpum peṟṟut, taṉ mutalum
   iṟutiyuṅ keṭṭu, iyalpum muṭamumāka muṭintu karutappaṭṭa cantiyum
   cārttum peṟṟum peṟātum varum" eṉṟum kūṟuvar arumpatavurai yāciriyar.
   varippāṭṭukkaḷ tiṇai nilaivari, kiṇainilai vari eṉavum mukamuṭai vari,
   mukamil vari, paṭaippu vari eṉavum avai palavākavum pākupāṭeytum.
   avaṟṟiṉiyalpellām piṉṉar, kāṉalvariyuḷḷum vēṉiṟkātaiyuḷḷum
   viḷaṅkaluṟum.
   iṉi, cantiyeṉpatu nāṭṭiyak kaṭṭuraiyiṉ pirivukaḷ oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarntu
   niṟkum nilai. atu, mukam, piratimukam, karuppam, viḷaivu, tuyttal eṉa
   aivakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ mukamāvatu eḻuvakaippaṭṭa uḻaviṉāṟ
   camaikkappaṭṭa pūḻiyuḷ iṭṭa vittup paruvañ ceytu muḷaittu muṭivatu
   pōlvatu: piratimukamāvatu aṅṅaṉam muḷaittal mutalāy ilaitōṉṟi nāṟṟāy
   muṭivatu pōlvatu;
   karuppamāvatu an nāṟṟu mutalāyk karuviruntu perukit taṉṉuṭ poruḷ
   potintu karuppamuṟṟi niṟpatu pōlvatu; viḷaivāvatu karuppa mutalāy
   virintu katir tiraṇṭiṭṭuk kāy tāḻntu muṟṟi viḷaintu muṭivatu pōlvatu;
   tuyttalāvatu viḷaiyappaṭṭa poruḷai aṟuttup pōriṭṭuk kaṭāviṭṭut tūṟṟip
   policeytu koṇṭu pōy uṇṭu makiḻvatu pōlvatu. vittu, nāṟṟu, karu,
   viḷaivu, tuyttal eṉpaṉa oṉṟiṉoṉṟu toṭarntu muṭitalpōla nāṭakattiṟkuriya
   katai aintu pakutiyāy oṉṟiṉoṉṟu toṭarntu muṭiyavēṇṭum eṉṟapaṭi.
   ivvuṟuppu,nāṭakattiṟ kiṉṟiyamaiyāta toṉṟeṉka.
   cētam eṉpatu ātik kataiyai āriyam, tamiḻ eṉṉum iruvakaik kūttiṟkēṟpac
   cētittiṭuvatu eṉpar.
   "āriyan tamiḻeṉuñ cīrnaṭa miraṇṭiṉum
       ātik kataiyai yavaṟṟiṟ koppac
       cētit tiṭuvatu cētameṉ ṟākum"

   eṉpatu kāṇka. "vilakkiṉiṟ puṇarttu" eṉpataṉāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭa
   vilakkuṟuppukkaḷ itukāṟum uṇarttappaṭṭaṉa.
   14. patiṉōrāṭalāvaṉa : acuraraik kolla amararāṭiya patiṉoru kūttukkaḷ.
   avaṟṟai,
   "kaṭaiyamayi rāṇimarak kālvintai kantaṉ
       kuṭaituṭimā valliyamaṟ kumpam-cuṭarviḻiyāṟ
       paṭṭamataṉ pēṭutirup pāvaiyaraṉ pāṇṭaraṅkam
       koṭṭiyivai kāṇpatiṉōr kūttu"

   eṉpataṉālaṟika. ivai niṉṟāṭal, paṭintāṭal eṉa iruvakaiyiṉa.
   "alliyaṅ koṭṭi kuṭaikuṭam pāṇṭaraṅkam
       malluṭa ṉiṉṟāṭa lāṟu"
       " tuṭikaṭaiyam pēṭu marakkālē pāvai
       vaṭivuṭaṉ vīḻntāṭa laintu."

   ikkūttu ovvoṉṟum evvekkāraṇam paṟṟi evvevviṭattu nikaḻttappaṭṭaṉa
   veṉpatu piṉṉark kaṭalāṭu kātaiyāl viḷakkamām.
   14. pāṭṭu eṉṟatu aka nāṭakaṅkaḷukkum, puṟanāṭakaṅkaḷukku muriya
   urukkaḷai. aka nāṭakaṅkaḷukkuriya uruvāvaṉa: kanta mutal piravantavuru
   vīṟāka irupatteṭṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ, kantameṉpatu aṭi varaiyaṟai yuṭaittāy oru
   tāḷattāṟ puṇarppatu; pirapantameṉpatu aṭivaraiyaṟaiyiṉṟip pala tāḷattāṟ
   puṇarppatu. puṟanāṭakaṅkaḷukkuriya uruvāvaṉa: tēvapāṇi mutalāka
   araṅkoḻi ceyyuḷīṟākac centuṟai vikaṟpaṅka ḷellāmeṉka.
   14. koṭṭu – koṭṭappaṭum vācciyaṅkaḷ. avai kītāṅkam, niruttāṅkam,
   upayāṅkam eṉpaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ kītāṅkam kītattiṟku vācippatu; niruttāṅkam
   niruttattiṟku vācippatu; upayāṅkam iraṇṭiṟkum vācippatu.
   15. koḷkai-nūl, āṅku : acai.
   16. āṭal – muṟkūṟiya akakkūttilum puṟakkūttiṉumuḷḷa āṭal. kīṟṟu,
   kaṭicari mutalākiya,tēcikkuriya kālkaḷ irupattu nāṉkum, cuṟṟutal,
   eṟital mutalākiya vaṭukiṟkuriya kālkaḷ patiṉāṉkum, uṭaṟṟūkku mutalākiya
   uṭalavarttaṉai oṉpatum akkūttuk kuriyaṉa "ciṅkaḷam iruvakai nilaiyiṉum
   eytum" eṉpārum, uṭalavarttaṉaiyaic ciṅkaḷa meṉpārumuḷar. eṉavē, tamiḻ,
   vaṭuku, ciṅkaḷam eṉa āṭal mūvakaippaṭum eṉka.
   16. pāṭal – iṉpam, teḷivu, niṟai, oḷi, vaṉcol, iṟuti, mantam, uccam
   eṉṉum eṇvakaip payaṉumuṭaiya pāṭal.
   16. pāṇi-tāḷam : atu koṭṭu, acai, tūkku, aḷavu eṉa nāṉku
   nilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiyatu; koṭṭāvatu amukkutal; acaiyāvatu tākkiyeḻutal;
   tūkkāvatu tākkit tūkkutal; aḷavāvatu tākkiṉa vōcai nērē mūṉṟu māttirai
   peṟumaḷavum varutal, koṭṭu arai māttirai: ataṟku vaṭivu, ka; acai oru
   māttirai; ataṟku vaṭivu, u. aḷavu mūṉṟu māttirai; ataṟku vaṭivu ḵ eṉak
   koḷka. koṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ vaṭivaik kuṟikka, ‘ka’ mutaliya iṭukuṟiyāka
   niṟuttappeṟṟaṉa veṉka.
   "koṭṭum acaiyun tūkkum aḷavum
       oṭṭap puṇarppatu pāṇi yākum"
       "kakaraṅ koṭṭē ekaram acaiyē
       ukaran tūkkē aḷavē yāytam"

   eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
   arai māttiraiyuṭaiya ēkatāḷam mutalākap patiṉāṟu māttiraiyuṭaiya
   pārvati lōcaṉam īṟākac coṉṉa nāṟpattoru tāḷam puṟakkūttukkuriya
   veṉavum, āṟaṉ maṭṭam eṉpaṉavum, eṭṭaṉ maṭṭam eṉpaṉavum, tāḷavoriyal
   eṉpaṉavum, taṉinilai yoriyal eṉpaṉavum, oṉṟaṉ pāṇi eṇkūttup pāṇiyīṟākak
   kiṭanta patiṉorupāṇi vikaṟpaṅkaḷum, mutalnaṭai, vāram mutalāyiṉavum
   akakkūttiṟkuriya veṉavum kūṟuvar.
   16. tūkku-ittāḷaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻivarum eḻuvakaippaṭṭa tūkkukkaḷ; avai
   centūkku, matalaittūkku, tuṇiputtūkku, kōyiṟṟūkku, nivapput tūkku,
   kaḻāṟṟūkku, neṭuntūkku eṉpaṉa; eṉṉai? orucīr centūkki rucīr matalai,
   muccīr tuṇipu, nāṟcīr kōyil, aiñcīr nivappē yaṟucīr kaḻālē, eḻucīr
   neṭuntūk keṉmaṉār pulavar" eṉṟārākaliṉ.
   17. koḷuttutal – āṭṭuvittal.
   18. piṇṭi-oṉṟu; īṇṭu oṟṟaikkaikku ākupeyar. piṇaiyal-iṇaital; īṇṭu
   iraṭṭaikkai. piṇṭi, oṟṟaikkai, iṇaiyāviṉaikkai eṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa;
   piṇaiyal, iṇaikkai, iraṭṭaikkai eṉpaṉa oru poruḷaṉa. avinayakkai
   iṅṅaṉam iruvakaippaṭum. piṇṭi-poruṭkaiyām; piṇaiyal-toḻiṟkaiyām eṉka.
   avaṟṟuḷ oṟṟaikkai muppattu mūṉṟu vakaippaṭum. avai: patākai-1,
   tiripatākai-2, kattarikai-3, tūpam-4, arāḷam-5, iḷampiṟai-6,
   cukatuṇṭam-7, muṭṭi-8, kaṭakam-9, cūci-10, kamala kōcikam-11,
   kāṅkūlam-12, kapittam-13, viṟpaṭi-14, kuṭaṅkai-15, alāpattiram-16,
   piramaram-17, tāmpiracūṭam-18, pacākam-19, mukuḷam-20, piṇṭi-21,
   terinilai-22, meynnilai-23, uṉṉam-24, maṇṭalam-25, caturam-26,
   māṉṟalai-27, caṅku-28, vaṇṭu-29, ilatai-30, kapōtam-31, makara
   mukam-32, valampuri-33 eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ,
   patākaiyāvatu nāṉku viralum tammuḷ oṭṭi nimirap peruviral kuñcittu
   niṟpatu.
   tiripatākai yāvatu patākaikkaiyiṉ aṇiviral muṭakkuvatu.
   kattarikaiyāvatu tiripatākaiyiṉ muṭaṅkiya aṇiviraṟ puṟattatākiya
   naṭuviralaic cuṭṭuviralōṭu porunta nimirppatu.
   tūpamāvatu nimirnta naṭuviralum cuṭṭu viralum pātippaṭa vaḷaiya
   niṟpatu.
   arāḷamāvatu peruviral kuñcittuc cuṭṭuviral muṭakki oḻinta viraṉ mūṉṟum
   nimirntu vaḷaivatu.
   iḷampiṟaiyāvatu cuṭṭuviralum naṭuviralum aṇiviralaiyum ciṟu viralaiyum
   oṭṭi akam vaḷaiya vaḷaitta peruviral avaṟṟai viṭṭu nīṅkuvatu. "cuṭṭum
   pēṭum anāmikai ciṟuviral, oṭṭi yakam vaḷaiya vocitta peruviral, viṭṭu
   nīṅkum vitiyiṟ ṟeṉpa."
   cukatuṇṭam "cukatuṇṭa meṉpatu toḻilpeṟak kiḷappiṉ, cuṭṭuviralum
   peruviral tāṉum oṭṭi yukirnuṉai kavvi muṉvaḷain tanāmikai muṭaṅkap
   pēṭṭoṭu ciṟuviral, tāṉmika nimirnta takutit teṉpa."
   anāmikai-aṇiviral; pavuttiraviral. pēṭu-naṭuviral.
   muṭṭi : "muṭṭi yeṉpatu moḻiyuṅ kālaic, cuṭṭu naṭuviral anāmikai
   ciṟuviral, iṟuka muṭakki yivaṟṟiṉmicaip peruviral, muṟukap piṭitta
   muṟaimait teṉpa."
   kaṭakam : kaṭaka mukamē karutuṅ kālaip, peruvira ṉuṉiyuñ cuṭṭuvira
   ṉuṉiyu, maruva vaḷaintavav vukirnuṉi kauvi, yoḻinta mūṉṟum vaḻivaḻi
   nimira, moḻintaṉa reṉpa muṭipaṟin tōrē."
   cūci : "cūci yeṉpatu tuṇiyuṅ kālai naṭuviral peruvira leṉṟivai tammi,
   ṉaṭaivuṭa ṉoṟṟic cuṭṭuvira ṉimira, voḻintaṉa vaḻivaḻi muṭaṅki niṟpa,
   moḻintaṉar mātō muṭipaṟin tōrē."
   patuma kōcikam : "patuma kōcikam pakaruṅkālai, yoppak kaivaḷait taintu
   viralu, meyayppaṭa vakaṉṟa vitiyiṟ ṟākum.
   kāṅkūlamāvatu : "kāṅkūlammē karutuṅ kālaic, cuṭṭum pēṭum peruviral
   mūṉṟu moṭṭimuṉ kuviya vanāmikai muṭaṅkic, ciṟu vira ṉimirnta ceykait
   tākum" "mukiḻkāṅ kūla muntuṟamoḻinta, kuvikāṅ kūlaṅ kuviviḻan tatuvē."
   "malarkāṅ kūla matumalarntatuvē."
   kapittamāvatu: "cuṭṭuvira ṉuṉiyum peruviraṉuṉiyum ukirnuṉai kauvip
   piṭitta oḻinta mūṉṟu viralum melleṉap piṭippatu.
   viṟpiṭi : "viṟpiṭi yeṉpatu virikkuṅ kālaic, cuṭṭoṭu pēṭiyatāmikai
   ciṟuvira, loṭṭi yakappāl vaḷaiyap peruviral, viṭṭu nimirum
   vitiyiṟṟākum".
   kuṭaṅkaiyāvatu ellā viraluṅ kūṭṭi uṭkuḻippatu.
   alāpattiramāvatu ciṟuviral mutalākiya vaintum vaḷaintu maṟivatu.
   piramaramāvatu anāmikai viralum naṭuviralum tammiṟ porunti valañcāyap
   peruviral naṭuviraliṉuḷḷē cērac cuṭṭu viralum ciṟuviralum piṉpē
   vaḷaintu niṟpatu.
   tāmpiracūṭamāvatu naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum peruviralum. tammil
   nuṉiyottuk kūṭi vaḷaintu ciṟuviralum aṇiviralum muṭaṅki nimirvatu.
   pacācamāvatu peruviralum cuṭṭuviralumaṉṟi oḻinta mūṉṟu viralum tammiṟ
   polintu niṟpateṉak koḷka.
   ap pacācantāṉ mūṉṟu vakaippaṭum:
   akanilaip pacācam-cuṭṭuvira ṉuṉiyil peruvira lakappaṭuvatu.
   mukanilaip pacācam- avviraṉ mukaṅkūṭi ukirviṭṭu niṟpatu.
   ukirnilaip pacācam-cuṭṭuviralum peruviralum ukir nuṉai kauvi niṟpatu.
   mukuḷamāvatu aintuviralun tammil talaikuvintu uyarntu niṟpatu.
   piṇṭiyeṉṟatu cuṭṭuviral pēṭiviral anāmikai ciṟuviral oṭṭi nekiḻa
   muṭaṅka avaṟṟiṉmēlē kuṟukkiṭap peruviral kaṭṭi vilaṅki viralvaḻi
   muṟaiyoṟṟal.
   terinilaiyāvatu ellāviralum virintu kuñcittu niṟpatu.
   meyn nilaiyāvatu ciṟuviralum aṇiviralum naṭuviralum cuṭṭu viralum
   viṭṭunimirac cuṭṭuviraṉmēṟ peruviral cēravaippatu.
   uṉṉamāvatu ciṟuviralum peruviralum tammuṭkūṭa oḻinta mūṉṟu viralum
   viṭṭu nimirvatu.
   maṇṭalam : "maṇṭalameṉpatu mācaṟak kiḷappiṟ, pēṭu nuṉiyum peruvira
   ṉuṉiyuṅ, kūṭi vaḷaintutam mukir nuṉai kauvi, yoḻinta mūṉṟu mokka
   viḷaivateṉa, moḻintaṉa reṉpa muḻutuṇarntōrē."
   caturamāvatu cuṭṭu viralum naṭuviralum aṇiviralum tammuṭ cērntu
   iṟaiñcap peruviral akam tara vaittuṭ ciṟuviral piṉpē nimirntu niṟpatu.
   māṉṟalaiyāvatu peruviraluñ ciṟuviralu moḻinta mūṉṟun tammil ottu oṉṟi
   muṉṉē iṟaiñci niṟpatu.
   caṅkamāvatu peruviral nimira oḻinta nāṉkuviralum vaḷaintu niṟpatu.
   vaṇṭāvatu peruviralum aṇiviralum vaḷaintu nuṉiyoṉṟic ciṟuviral nimirntu
   cuṭṭuviralum naṭuviralum nekiḻa vaḷaintu niṟpatu.
   ilataiyāvatu naṭuviralum cuṭṭuviralum kūṭinimirap peruviral avaṟṟiṉ
   kīḻvarai cēra oḻinta iraṇṭu viralum vaḻimuṟai piṉṉē nimirntu niṟpatu.
   kapōta meṉṟatu patākaik kaiyiṟ peruviral viṭṭu nimirvatu.
   makaramukamāvatu peruviralum cuṭṭuviralum nimirntukūṭa oḻinta
   mūṉṟuviralun tammuḷoṉṟi ataṟku vēṟāy niṟpatu.
   valampuriyāvatu, ciṟuviralum peruviralum nimirntu cuṭṭuviraliṉ akam
   vaḷaintu oḻinta viraṇṭum nimirntu iṟaiñci niṟpatu.
   iṉi, iraṭṭaikkai patiṉaintu vakaippaṭum. avai: añcali.1, puṭpāñcali-2,
   patumāñcali-3, kapōtam-4, kaṟkaṭakam-5, cuvattikam-6, kaṭakāvaruttam-7,
   niṭatam-8, tōram-9, uṟcaṅkam-⁰, puṭpapuṭam-11, makaram-12, cayantam-13,
   apayavattam-14, varuttamāṉam-15, ivaṟṟuḷ,
   añcaliyāvatu : iraṇṭu kaiyum patākaiyāy akamoṉṟuvatu.
   puṭpāñcaliyāvatu iraṇṭu kaiyum kuṭaṅkaiyāy vantu oṉṟuvatu.
   kapōtameṉṟatu iraṇṭu kaiyum kapōtamākak kūṭṭuvatu.
   kaṟkaṭaka meṉṟatu terinilaik kaiyiraṇṭum aṅkuli piṇaintu varuvatu.
   cuvattika meṉṟatu maṇikkaṭṭiṟ poruntiya patākaiyiraṇṭaiyum maṇikkaṭṭi
   lēṟṟi vaippatu.
   kaṭakā varuttamāvatu iraṇṭu kaiyum kaṭakamāy maṇikkaṭṭukku ēṟa iyaintu
   niṟpatu.
   niṭatamāvatu muṭṭiyāka iraṇṭu kaiyum camañceyvatu.
   tōramāvatu iraṇṭu kaiyum patākaiyākki akampura moṉṟi muṉtāḻntu niṟpatu.
   uṟcaṅkamāvatu orukai piṟaikkaiyākak koṇṭu orukai arāḷamākki iraṇṭu
   kaiyum maṇikkaṭṭi lēṟṟi vaippatu.
   puṭpapuṭamāvatu kuṭaṅkaiyiraṇṭum tammiṟ pakkaṅ kāṭṭi niṟpatu.
   makarameṉṟatu kapōta miraṇṭu kaiyum akampuṟa moṉṟa vaippatu.
   cayantam (viṭupaṭṭatu)
   apayamattamāvatu. irukaiyum kaituṇṭamāka neñcuṟa nōkki nekiḻntu
   niṟpatu.
   varuttamāṉamāvatu mukuḷak kaiyiṟ kapōtak kaiyai etiriṭṭuc cērppatu.
   18- eḻiṟkai-aḻakupeṟak kāṭṭuṅ kai. toḻiṟkai-toḻil peṟak kāṭṭuṅkai.
   poruṭkai yeṉpatum varuvikkap paṭum; āvatu, poruḷuṟak kāṭṭuṅ kai.
   19. koṇṭavakai yaṟitalāvatu-piṇṭiyum piṇaiyalum puṟakkuttuk kuriyakai
   yeṉṟum; eḻiṟkaiyum toḻiṟkaiyum akakkūttukkuriya kai yeṉṟum aṟital.
   20-1. kūṭai, vāram eṉpaṉa cila tāḷavikaṟpaṅkaḷukkum, icaippāṭṭukkaḷiṉ
   iyakkaṅkaḷukkum, avinayakkaikaṭkum, piṟa cilavaṟṟiṟkum peyar, īṇṭu
   avinayak kaikaḷai yuṇartti niṉṟaṉa.
   kūṭai-oṟṟaikkai; iraṭṭai yoṟṟaik kaiyākiya kuvitta kaiyaiyum itu
   kuṟikkum. vāram-iraṭṭaikkai. akakkūttu nikaḻumiṭattu oṟṟaiyiṟ ceyta
   kaittoḻil iraṭṭaiyiṟ pukāmalum iraṭṭaiyiṟceyta kaittoḻil oṟṟaiyiṟ
   pukāmalum kaḷaital. iṉṉum tēciyiṟ kaittoḻil mārkkattup pukāmalum,
   mārkkattuk kaittoḻil tēciyiṟ pukāmalum kaḷaitaleṉṟumām; oṟṟaiyum
   iraṭṭaiyum tēcik kūṟākalāṉum iraṭṭaiyum iraṭṭaik kiraṭṭaiyum vaṭukiṟ
   kūṟākalāṉu meṉka.
   22-3. piṇṭi-poruṭkai, āṭal-piṇaiyal; toḻiṟkai puṟakkūttil āṭal
   nikaḻumiṭattu avinayam nikaḻāmalum, avinayam nikaḻumiṭattu āṭal
   nikaḻāmalum kaḷaital.
   24- kuravai-kuravaikkūttu; atu kāmamum veṉṟiyum poruḷākak kuravaic
   ceyyuḷ pāṭṭāka eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉmarēṉum kaipiṇaittāṭuvatu;
   ataṉ iyalpiṉai, "kuravai yeṉpatu kūṟuṅ kālaic, ceytōr ceyta kāmamum
   viṟalum, eyta vuraikku miyalpiṟ ṟeṉpa" eṉpataṉā ṉaṟika.
   vari-varikkūttu: aḵtu avaravar piṟanta nilattaṉmaiyum piṟappiṟkēṟṟa
   toḻiṟ ṟaṉmaiyum tōṉṟa naṭittal. itaṟku varicceyyuḷ pāṭṭākum. ṭuvatu
   vakukkuṅ kālaip, piṟanta nilaṉuñ ciṟanta toḻilum, aṟiyak kūṟi yāṟṟuḻi
   vaḻaṅkal" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. varikkūttu oruvar perumpālum vēṟṟurut tāṅki
   naṭippatu.
   it toṭarnilaic ceyyuḷiṉkaṇ, mullai cārntu āycciyar kuravaiyum, kuṟiñci
   cārntu kuṉṟakkuravaiyum, neytal cārntu kāṉalvariyum, pālai cārntu
   vēṭṭuva variyum , marutañ cārntu ūr cūḻvariyum nikaḻntaṉavāka
   amaintiruttal aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu.
   varikkūttāṉatu kaṇkūṭuvari, kāṉalvari, uḷvari, puṟavari, kiḷarvari,
   tērccivari, kāṭcivari, eṭuttukkōḷvari eṉa eṇvakaippaṭum.
   ivaṟṟiṉiyalpellām vēṉiṟ kātaiyāl viḷakkamām. iṉi, " ivvariyeṉpataṉaip
   palvarik kūtteṉpārumuḷar" eṉṟu kūṟi, aṭiyārkku nallār ataṟkeṭuttuk
   kāṭṭiya kaliveṇpāṭṭoṉṟāl tamiḻakattē paṇṭai nāḷil vaḻaṅkiya ettaṉaiyō
   vakaiyāṉa kūttukkaḷum pāṭṭukkaḷum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa: acceyyuḷ piṉvarumāṟu;
   " cittup piḻukkai yuṭaṉcanti yōrmulai
       konti kavuci kuṭappiḻukkai-kattaṉpāṭ
       ṭālaṅkāṭ ṭāṇṭi parumaṇa ṉellicci
       cūlan tarunaṭṭan tūṇṭiluṭaṉ-cīlamikum
       āṇṭi yamaṇpuṉavē ṭāḷatti kōppāḷi
       pāṇṭip piḻukkaiyuṭaṉ pāmpāṭṭi-mīṇṭa
       kaṭavuṭ caṭaivīra mākēcaṅ kāmaṉ
       makiḻcintu vāmaṉa rūpam-vikaṭaneṭum
       pattiraṅ koṟṟi palakaivāḷ papparappeṇ
       tattacam pāram takuṇiccaṅ-- kattu
       muṟaiyīṇ ṭiruñcittu muṇṭita maṉṉap
       paṟaipaṇ ṭitaṉpuṭpa pāṇam-iṟaiparavu
       pattaṉ kuravaiyē pappaṟai kāvataṉ
       pittaṉoṭu māṇi perumpiḻukkai-ettuṟaiyum
       ēttivaruṅ kaṭkaḷi yāṇṭu viḷaiyāṭṭuk
       kōtta paṟaikkuṭumpu kōṟkūttu-mūtta
       kiḻavaṉ kiḻaviyē kiḷḷup piṟāṇṭi
       aḻakuṭaiya paṇṇivika ṭāṅkan tikaḻcempoṉ
       ammaṉai pantu kaḻaṅkāṭa lālikkum
       viṇṇakak kāḷi viṟaṟkonti-allāta
       vāynta taṉivaṇṭu vāricci picciyuṭaṉ
       cānta muṭaiya caṭātāri – ēyntaviṭai
       takkapiṭār nirttan taḷippāṭṭuc cāturaṅkan
       tokka toḻilpuṉainta cōṇāṇṭu mikka
       malaiyāḷi vētāḷi vāṇi kutirai
       cilaiyāṭu vēṭu civapput talaiyil
       tiruviḷakkup picci tirukkuṉ ṟayiṟpeṇ
       ṭiruṇmukattup pētai yiruḷaṉ-porumukattup
       pallāṅ kuḻiyē pakaṭi pakavatiyāṉ
       nallārtan tōḷvīccu naṟcāḻal-allāta
       unti yavaliṭi yūrāḷi yōkiṉicci
       kuntivarum pāraṉ kuṇalaikkūt-tantiyampō
       tāṭuṅ kaḷikoyyu muḷḷippū vaiyaṉukkup
       pāṭumpāṭ ṭāṭum paṭupaḷḷi-nāṭaṟiyuṅ
       kumpīṭu nāṭṭaṅ kuṇāṭṭaṅ kuṇālaiyē
       tuñcāta cummaippūc cōṉaka-mañcari
       ēṟṟa vuḻaimai paṟaimaimuta leṉṟeṇṇik
       kōttavarik kūttiṉ kulam"

   --------------------
   (icaiyāciriyaṉ amaiti)
   26-36. yāḻum kuḻalum cīrum miṭaṟum
       tāḻkural taṇṇumai āṭaloṭu ivaṟṟiṉ
       icainta pāṭal icaiyuṭaṉ paṭuttu
       varikkum āṭaṟkum uripporuḷ iyakkit
       tēcikat tiruviṉ ōcai kaṭaippiṭittut
       tēcikat tiruviṉ ōcai ellām
       ācuiṉṟu uṇarnta aṟiviṉaṉ ākik
       kaviyatu kuṟippum āṭal tokutiyum
       pakutip pāṭalum koḷuttuṅ kālai
       vacaiaṟu kēḷvi vakuttaṉaṉ virikkum
       acaiyā marapiṉ icaiyōṉ tāṉum

   yāḻum kuḻalum cīrum miṭaṟum tāḻkural taṇṇumai āṭaloṭu-yāḻppāṭalum
   vaṅkiyap pāṭalum tāḷak kūṟupāṭukaḷum miṭaṟṟup pāṭalum mantamākiya
   curattiṉaiyuṭaiya taṇṇumaiyum kūttukkaḷum vallaṉāy, ivaṟṟiṉ icainta
   pāṭal icaiyuṭaṉ paṭuttu-ivaṟṟuṭaṉē cēracceyta urukkaḷai icai
   koḷḷumpaṭiyum cuvai poruntumpaṭiyum puṇarkkavum vallaṉāy, varikkum
   āṭaṟkkum uripporuḷ iyakki-centuṟai veṇṭuṟai eṉṉum iruvakaippaṭṭa
   pāṭalkaḷukkum poruḷāṉa iyakkam nāṉkiṉaiyum amaittu, tēcikat tiruviṉ
   ōcai kaṭaippiṭittu-aḻakuṭaiya tēcāntaraṅkaḷiṉ pāṭaikaḷaiyum aṟintu,
   tēcikat tiruviṉ ōcaiyellām āciṉṟu uṇarnta aṟiviṉaṉ āki-antap pāṭaikaḷ
   icai pūṇumpaṭiyaiyum aṟintu, kaviyatu kuṟippum āṭaṟṟokutiyum
   pakutippāṭalum koḷuttuṅkālai-iyaṟpulavaṉ karuttum nāṭakap pulavaṉ
   īṭuvaravukaḷum avaṟṟuk kaṭaitta pāṭalkaḷum tammiṟ cantippikku miṭattu,
   vacaiaṟu kēḷvi vakuttaṉaṉ virikkum-kuṟṟam tīrnta nūl vaḻakkālē
   vakukkavum virikkavum vallaṉāyuḷḷa, acaiyā marapiṉ icaiyōṉ
   tāṉum-taḷarāta iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya icaippulavaṉum;
   26. yāḻ-pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ eṉa yāḻ
   nālvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟukku narampu muṟaiyē irupattoṉṟum, pattoṉpatum,
   patiṉāṉkum, ēḻumākum. eṉṉai?
   "oṉṟu mirupatu moṉpatum pattuṭaṉē
       niṉṟa patiṉāṉkum piṉṉēḻum-kuṉṟāta
       nālvakai yāḻiṟku naṉṉarampu coṉmuṟaiyē
       mēlvakai nūlōr viti"

   eṉpavākaliṉ, ōrēḻmēṟpattuṭaṉē eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ makarayāḻiṟku narampu
   patiṉēḻākum. innālvakaiyaṉṟic ciṟupāṉmaiyāṉ varum yāḻ piṟavu muḷa
   eṉpar. yāḻiṉ piṟavilakkaṇaṅkaḷ piṉ kāṉalvari yuraiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum.
   yāḻ-yāḻppāṭal eṉka.
   26. kuḻal-kuḻaliṉ pāṭal eṉka. kuḻal-vaṅkiyam; atu mūṅkil, cantaṉam,
   veṇkalam, ceṅkāli, karuṅkāli yeṉṉum aintāṉum iyaṟṟappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ
   mūṅkilē mutaṉmaiyāṉatu; atu paṟṟiyē pullāṅkuḻal, vēyṅkuḻal, vaṅkiyam,
   vēṇu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ kuḻalukku vaḻaṅkuva vāyiṉa. mūṅkil uttamam,
   veṇkalam mattimam, ēṉaiya atamam eṉpar. ik kālattuk karuṅkāli ceṅkāli
   cantaṉam ivaṟṟāṟ koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum, ivai koḷḷumiṭattu, uyarnta
   ottanilattiṟ peruka vaḷarntu nālu kāṟṟum mayaṅkiṉ nātamillai yātalāl,
   mayaṅkā nilattilē iḷamaiyum neṭum pirāyamumiṉṟi oru puruṭāyup pukka
   periya marattai veṭṭi, oru puruṭākāramākac ceytu, ataṉai niḻalilēyiṭṭu
   āṟavaittu, tirukutal piḷattal pōḻntu paṭutal iṉmaiyaṟintu oru yāṇṭu
   ceṉṟapiṉ vaṅkiyam ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, itaṉ nīḷam irupatu viralaḷavum,
   cuṟṟu nālarai viralaḷavumām eṉavum; itu tuḷaiyiṭumiṭattu nellariciyil
   oru pāti maraṉiṟuttik kaṭaintu veṇkalattāl aṇaicu paṇṇi
   iṭamukattaiyaṭaittu valamukam veḷiyāka viṭappaṭum eṉavum, itilē
   tūpamukattiṉ iraṇṭu viral nīkki mutalvāy viṭṭu, immutal vāykku
   ēḻaṅkulamviṭṭu vaḷaivāyiṉum iraṇṭu nīkki naṭuviṉiṉṟa oṉpatu viraliṉum
   eṭṭut tuḷaiyiṭappaṭum eṉavum, tuḷaikaḷiṉ iṭaipparappu oru viralakalam
   koḷḷappaṭum eṉavum kūṟuvar. itaṉai vācikkumiṭattu, vaḷaivāy cērnta
   tuḷaiyai muttiraiyeṉṟu nīkki, muṉṉiṉṟa ēḻu tuḷaiyiṉum iṭakkaiyiṉ
   iṭaimūṉṟu viralum, valakkaiyiṉ peruviraloḻinta nāṉku viralum paṟṟi
   vācikkappaṭum. iv vaṅkiyattiṉ ēḻu tuḷaikaḷilum carikamapatani eṉṉum
   ēḻeḻuttiṉaiyum māttiraippaṭuttit toḻil ceyya ivaṟṟuḷḷē ēḻicaiyum
   piṟakkum; ivai piṟantu ivaṟṟuḷḷē paṇkaḷ piṟakkum;
   "carika mapataniyeṉ ṟēḻeḻuttāṟ ṟāṉam
       variparanta kaṇṇiṉāy vaittut –terivariya
       ēḻicaiyun tōṉṟu mivaṟṟuḷḷē paṇpiṟakkum
       cūḻmutalāñ cuttat tuḷai"

   eṉpatu kāṇka.
   26. cīr-akakkūttiṟkum,puṟakkūttiṟku muriya iruvakait tāḷak kūṟupāṭukaḷ.
   26. miṭaṟu-miṭaṟṟuppāṭal. itaṉaic cārīravīṇaiyeṉpar. puṟkalam eṉappaṭum
   uṭampu aimpūtaṅkaḷiṉ pariṇāmattāl ākumuṟaimai aṭiyārkku nallārāl
   viritturaikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu; aḵtu caṇṭaikku vēṇṭappaṭuvatiṉṟu.
   uṭampiṉaḷavu taṉ kaiyāl toṇṇūṟṟāṟu aṅkulam eṉavum avaṟṟuḷ mēlē
   nāṟpattēḻarai yaṅkulamum kīḻē nāṟpattēḻarai yaṅkulamum viṭṭu naṭuniṉṟa
   ōraṅkulam mūlātāram eṉavum, mūlātāran toṭaṅki eḻuttiṉ nātam āḷattiyāyp
   piṉ icai yeṉṟum paṇṇeṉṟum peyarām eṉavum, neñcum miṭaṟum nākkum mūkkum
   aṇṇākkum utaṭum pallum talaiyum eṉṉum peruntāṉam eṭṭiṉum eṭuttal
   paṭuttal nalital kampitam kuṭilam oli uruṭṭu tākku eṉṉuṅ kiriyaika
   ḷeṭṭāṉum paṇṇip paṭuttalāṟ paṇṇeṉṟu peyarāyiṟṟu eṉavum kūṟuvar.
   āḷatti yeṉpatu ikkālattu ālāpaṉam eṉa vaḻaṅkappeṟum. atu makara oṟṟuṭaṉ
   kūṭiya kuṟṟeḻuttālum, neṭṭeḻuttālum ‘teṉṉā, teṉā, teṉṉā, teṉā’ eṉṉum
   acaikaḷ kūṭṭiyum ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, maṟṟum: ma,na,ta, eṉṉum mūṉṟu
   oṟṟukkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya a i u e o eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟeḻut taintāṉum ā ī ū ē ō eṉṉum
   neṭṭeḻut taintāṉum ceyyappaṭum eṉavum, ivvāḷattitāṉ accu, pāraṇai
   yeṉṟum, kāṭṭāḷatti, niṟavāḷatti, paṇṇāḷatti yeṉṟum eḻuttu vēṟṟumaiyāṟ
   peyareytum eṉavum, piṟavāṟum kūṟupa.
   27. taṇṇumai – piṟakaruvikaṭkum, upalakkaṇam.
   27. āṭal-akakkūttu, puṟakkūttu. patiṉōrāṭal eṉpaṉa.
   29. uripporuḷ-iyakkam; pāṭṭiṉ naṭai. atu mutaṉaṭai, vāram, kūṭai, tiraḷ
   eṉa nālvakaippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ, mutaṉaṭai mikattāḻnta celaviṉaiyuṭaiyatu;
   vāram colloḻukkamum icaiyoḻukkamum uṭaiyatu; kūṭai coṟceṟivum
   icaicceṟivum uṭaiyatu; tiraḷ mika muṭukiya naṭaiyiṉai yuṭaiyatu.
   icaiyuṭaṉ paṭuttu iyakkik kaṭaippiṭittu uṇarnta aṟiviṉaṉāki virikkum
   icaiyōṉ eṉka.
   ------------------
   (kaviñaṉ amaiti)
   37 - 44. imiḻkaṭal varaippil tamiḻakam aṟiyat
       tamiḻmuḻutu aṟinta taṉmaiyaṉ āki
       vēttiyal potuviyal eṉṟuiru tiṟattiṉ
       nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉku kaṭaippiṭittu
       icaiyōṉ vakkirit tiṭṭattai uṇarntuāṅku
       acaiyā marapiṉ atupaṭa vaittu
       māṟṟār ceyta vacaimoḻi aṟintu
       nāttolaivu illā naṉṉūl pulavaṉum,

   imiḻ kaṭal varaippil tamiḻakam aṟiya- olikkiṉṟa kaṭal cūḻnta puviyiṉ
   kaṇṇē tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉar aṟiya, tamiḻ muḻutu aṟinta taṉmaiyaṉ āki-
   muttamiḻum tuṟai pōkak kaṟṟuṇarnta taṉmaiyaiyuṭaiyaṉāki, vēttiyal
   potuviyal eṉṟu irutiṟattiṉ-vēttiya leṉṟum potuviyaleṉṟum kūṟappaṭum
   iraṇṭu kūṟupāṭṭiṉaiyuṭa, nāṭṭiya naṉṉūl naṉku kaṭaippiṭittu-nāṭaka
   nūlai naṉṟākap paṟṟik koṇṭu, icaiyōṉ vakkirit tiṭṭattai
   uṇarntu-icaippulavaṉ āḷatti vaitta paṇṇīrmaiyai aṟintu,
   āṅku-aṟintavaṇṇam; acaiyā marapiṉ-taḷarāta muṟaimaiyālē, atu paṭa
   vaittu-avaṉ tāḷanilaiyil eyta vaitta niṟam taṉ kaviyilē tōṉṟa vaikka
   vallaṉāy, māṟṟōr ceyta vacaimoḻi aṟintu nāttolaivu illā-muṉ pakaivar
   ceyta vacaimoḻikaḷaiyaṟintu avai tōṟṟātapaṭi vacaiyillāta moḻikaḷāl
   nāṭakakkavi ceyyavalla keṭāta nāviṉaiyuṭaiya, naṉaṉūṟ pulavaṉum-nalla
   nūlai valla pulavaṉum;
   37. tamiḻakam-vēṅkaṭam kumari tīmpuṉaṟ pauvam eṉpaṉa; vaṭakkum teṟkum
   kuṭakkum kuṇakkum ellaiyākavuṭaiya vaṇpukaḻ mūvar taṇpoḻil varaippu.
   41. vakkirittal-āḷatti ceytal, uṇarntu eṉpataṟku, mutalum muṟaiyum
   muṭivum niṟaiyum kuṟaiyum kiḻamaiyum valivum melivum camaṉum
   varaiyaṟaiyum nīrmaiyum eṉṉum patiṉoru pākupāṭṭiṉāṉum aṟintu eṉpar.
   taṉmaiyaṉākik kaṭaippiṭittu uṇarntu vaittu aṟintu tolaivillāta
   pulavaṉum eṉka.
   --------------------
   (taṇṇumaiyāciriyaṉ amaiti)
   45-55. āṭal pāṭal icaiyē tamiḻē
       paṇṇē pāṇi tūkkē muṭamē
       tēcikam eṉṟuivai āciṉ uṇarntu
       kūṭai nilattaik kuṟaivuiṉṟu mikuttuāṅku
       vāra nilattai vāṅkupu vāṅki
       vāṅkiya vārattu yāḻum kuḻalum
       ēṅkiya miṭaṟum icaivaṉa kēṭpak
       kūrukirk karaṇam kuṟiaṟintu cērtti
       ākkalum aṭakkalum mīttiṟam paṭāmaic
       cittirak karaṇam citaivuiṉṟi celuttum
       attaku taṇṇumai aruntoḻil mutalvaṉum

   āṭalpāṭal icaiyē tamiḻē-ellāk kūttukkaḷum ellāp pāṭṭukkaḷum ellā
   icaikaḷum iyal icai nāṭakameṉṉum mūvakait tamiḻkaḷum, paṇṇē pāṇi tūkkē
   muṭamē-ellāp paṇkaḷum iruvakait tāḷaṅkaḷum eḻuvakait tūkkukaḷum,
   ivaṟṟiṉ kuṟṟaṅkaḷum, tēcikam-iyaṟcol, tiricol ticaiccol, vaṭacol eṉṉum
   nālvakaic colvaḻakkukaḷum, eṉṟivai āciṉ uṇarntu-eṉṟu collappaṭṭa
   ivaṟṟai nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntu, kūṭai nilattaik kuṟaiviṉṟi mikuttu-ōruruvai
   iraṭṭikkiraṭṭi cērttaviṭattu nekiḻātapaṭi nirampa niṟuttavum, āṅku vāra
   nilattai vāṅkupu vāṅki-avviṭattup peṟum iraṭṭiyaip pākavuruvāṉavaḻi
   niṟkumāṉam niṟuttik kaḻiyumāṉaṅ kaḻikkavum vallaṉāy, vāṅkiya vārattu
   yāḻum kuḻalum ēṅkiya miṭaṟum icaivaṉa kēṭpa-ippaṭi nikaḻnta urukkaḷil
   yāḻppāṭalum kuḻaliṉpāṭalum kaṇṭappāṭalum icaintu naṭakkiṉṟapaṭi kēṭpōr
   cevikkoḷḷumāṟu, kūrukirk karaṇamkuṟi aṟintu cērtti-taṇṇumaiyai viraliṉ
   ceykaiyālē kuṟiyaṟintu cēra vācikkavallaṉāy, ākkalum aṭakkalum-maṟṟaik
   karuvikaḷiṉ kuṟaiyai nirapputalum mikutiyai aṭakkutalum, mīttiṟam
   paṭāmai-ākkumiṭattum aṭakkumiṭattum icaiyil irantiran tōṉṟāmaṟ
   ceytalum, cittirak karaṇam citaiviṉṟu celuttum-ivvaṉaittum
   ceyyumiṭattuk kaittoḻil aḻakupeṟac ceytu kāṭṭalum vallaṉāy, attaku
   taṇṇumai aruntoḻil mutalvaṉum-attaṉmaiyuṭaiya taṇṇumaik karuviyiṉ ariya
   toḻilaiyuṭaiya āciriyaṉum;
   45. icai-narappaṭaivāl uraikkappaṭṭa patiṉō rāyirattut toḷḷāyirattut
   toṇṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya ātiyicaikaḷ eṉpar. itaṟkup piramāṇamāka eṭuttuk kāṭṭap
   peṟṟatoru cūttiram aṭiyil varumāṟu citaintu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
   "uyiruyir meyyaḷa vuraittavaim pāliṉum
       uṭaṟami ḻiyalicai yēḻuṭaṉ pakuttu
       mūvēḻ peytan..................
       toṇṭu mīṇṭa paṉṉī rāyiraṅ
       koṇṭaṉa riyaṟṟal koḷaivallōr kaṭaṉē"

   45. tamiḻ eṉpataṟku, vaṭaveḻut torīi vanta eḻuttāṉē uṟaḻntu kāṭṭappaṭṭa
   vākkiyak kūṟukaḷ eṉṟum uraippar.
   46. paṇ-narappaṭaivāl niṟantōṉṟap paṇṇap paṭā niṉṟa paṇṇum, paṇṇiyaṟ
   ṟiṟamum, tiṟamum, tiṟattiṟamumām.
   46. muṭam-kuṟṟam
   47. ācu-nuṇmai. āciṉ nuṇarntu eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkuk kuṟṟamiṉṟi
   yuṇarnteṉpatu poruḷākum.
   51. ēṅkiya-olittalaiyuṭaiya
   52. kūrukir-kūriya nakam: viralukku ākupeyar. karaṇam-ceykai.
   55. taṇṇumai eṉṟatu ēṉait tōṟkaruvikaḷaiyum aṭakki niṉṟatu. avaṟṟai,
   "pērikai paṭakam iṭakkai uṭukkai
       cīrmiku mattaḷam callikai karaṭikai
       timilai kuṭamuḻāt takkai kaṇappaṟai
       tamarukam taṇṇumai tāvil taṭāri
       antari muḻavoṭu cantira vaḷaiyam
       montai muracē kaṇviṭu tūmpu
       nicāḷam tuṭumai ciṟupaṟai aṭakkam
       mācil takuṇiccam viralēṟu pākam
       tokka upāṅkam tuṭiperum paṟaiyeṉa
       mikka nūlōr viritturait taṉarē"

   eṉṉuñ cūttirattālaṟika. ivai akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu,
   puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇamai muḻavu, nāṇmuḻavu, kālai muḻavu eṉa
   eḻuvakaippaṭum eṉṟum, muṉcoṉṉa uttamamāṉa mattaḷam callikai iṭakkai
   karaṭikai pērikai paṭakam kuṭamuḻā eṉpaṉa akamuḻavum, mattimamāṉa
   taṇṇumai takkai takuṇiccam mutaliyaṉa akappuṟa muḻavum, atamak
   karuviyāṉa kaṇappaṟai mutaliyaṉa puṟamuḻavum, muṉ kūṟappaṭāta
   neytaṟpaṟai mutaliyaṉa puṟappuṟa muḻavum, muracu nicāḷam tuṭumai
   timilai yeṉṉum vīramuḻavu nāṉkum paṇṇamai muḻavum, nāḻikaip paṟaiyāṉatu
   nāṇmuḻavum, tuṭi eṉpatu kālai muḻavum ākumeṉṟum kūṟuvar. aṭikaḷ
   taṇṇumai yoṉṟaṉaiyē vitantōtutaliṉ, atuvē ēṉaik karuvikaṭkellām
   mutalāmeṉpatu peṟṟām.
   ---------------
   (kuḻalāciriyaṉ amaiti)
   56-69. colliya iyalpiṉiṟ cittira vañcaṉai
       pulliya aṟintu puṇarppōṉ paṇpiṉ
       varttaṉai nāṉkum mayalaṟap peytuāṅku
       ēṟṟiya kuraliḷi eṉṟuiru narampiṉ
       oppak kēṭkum uṇarviṉaṉ ākip
       paṇamai muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟi aṟintu
       taṇṇumai mutalvaṉ taṉṉoṭu porunti
       vaṇṇap paṭṭaṭai yāḻmēl vaittuāṅku
       icaiyōṉ pāṭiya icaiyiṉ iyaṟkai
       vantatu vaḷarttu varuvatu oṟṟi
       iṉpuṟa iyakki icaipaṭa vaittu
       vāra nilattaik kēṭuiṉṟu vaḷarttuāṅku
       īra nilattiṉ eḻuttueḻuttu āka
       vaḻuviṉṟu icaikkum kuḻalōṉ tāṉum,

   colliya iyalpiṉ-nūlkaḷiṟ coṉṉamuṟaimaiyālē, cittira vañcaṉai pulliya
   aṟintu-cittirap puṇarppum vañcaṉaip puṇarppum eṉṉum iru kūṟṟiṉaiyum
   aṟintu, puṇarppōṉ paṇpiṉ-puṇarkkavalla pāṭalāciriyaṉaiyotta
   aṟiviṉaiyuṭaiyaṉāki, varttaṉai nāṉkum mayal aṟappeytu-ārōkaṇa
   avarōkaṇaṅkaḷil viralkaḷai viṭṭup piṭikkum varttaṉai nāṉkiṉālum nūṟṟu
   mūṉṟu paṇṇīrmaikaḷaiyum tannilai kulaiyāmaṟ kāṭṭavallaṉāy,
   āṅku-avviṭattu, ēṟṟiya kural iḷi eṉṟu iru narampiṉ-patiṉāṟ
   kōvaiyiṉiṭattuk kural narampu iraṭṭikkavarum arumpālaiyaiyum, iḷi
   narampu iraṭṭikkavarum mēṟcempālaiyaiyayum, ivaipōla allāta
   pālaikaḷaiyum, oppakkēṭkum uṇarviṉaṉ āki-icainūl vaḻakkālē iṇai narampu
   toṭuttup pāṭum aṟiviṉaiyu muṭaiyaṉāy, paṇ amai muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟi
   aṟintu-paṇṇutalamainta muḻaviṉ kaṇṇeṟiyiṉai aṟintu, taṇṇumai mutalvaṉ
   taṉṉoṭum porunti-taṇṇumai mutalvaṉōṭum porunti, vaṇṇap paṭṭaṭai yāḻmēl
   vaittu āṅku-niṟattiṉaiyuṭaiya iḷi yeṉṉum narampiṉai yāḻmēl vaittu,
   icaiyōṉ pāṭiya icaiyiṉ iyaṟkai-ataṉvaḻiyē icaikkāraṉ pāṭiya
   pāṭṭiṉiyalpai, vantatu vaḷarttu-pāṭukiṉṟa paṇ varavukaḷukkuc curam
   kuṟaivu paṭāmai niṟutti, varuvatu oṟṟi-antap paṇṇukku ayal viravāmal
   nōkki, iṉpuṟa iyakki-vaṇṇa mutalākak kāṭṭappaṭṭa pāṭaliyal vaḻakkellām
   cuvai porunta nirappak kāṭṭi, icaipaṭa vaittu-muṟkūṟiya mutalum
   muṟaiyum mutalāṉa paṇṇilakkaṇam patiṉoṉṟiṉaiyum nirampavaittu, vāra
   nilattaik kēṭiṉṟu pārttu-vārappāṭalai aḷavu nirampa niṟuttavallaṉāy,
   āṅku-pāṭaliṭattu, īranilattiṉeḻuttu eḻuttāka-coṉṉīrmaikaḷiṉ eḻuttukkaḷ
   citaiyāmalē eḻutteḻuttāka icaikkum, vaḻuviṉiṉṟu icaikkum kuḻalōṉ
   tāṉum-iccollappaṭṭa iyalpukaḷai ilakkaṇappaṭi vaḻuvāmal vācittuk
   kāṭṭavalla kuḻalāciriyaṉum;
   56. cittirap puṇarppāvatu icaikoḷḷum eḻuttukkaḷiṉ mēlai valloṟṟu
   vantavaḻi melloṟṟup pōlap paṇṇīrmai niṟuttal. vañcaṉaippuṇarppāvatu
   icaikoḷḷā veḻuttukkaḷiṉ mēlē valloṟṟu vantavaḻi melloṟṟup pōla
   nekiḻttup puṇarttal.
   58. varttaṉai-kuḻalil viraluḷarntūtun tuḷaikaḷ cuṭṭuviral mutalāka
   viṭṭup piṭippatu ārōkaṇam; ciṟuviral mutalāka viṭṭuppiṭippatu
   avarōkaṇam; iṅṅaṉam ēṟṟiyiṟakkalum, iṟakkiyēṟṟalum varttaṉai
   yeṉappaṭum.
   59. ‘ēṟṟiya kuraliṉi....uṇarviṉaṉāki’ eṉpataṉ poruḷ piṉṉar yāḻāciriyaṉ
   amaiti kūṟumiṭattu uraikku muṟaiyāl viḷakkamām.
   63. paṭṭaṭai-aṭimaṇai; ellāp paṇṇiṟkum aṭimaṇai yātaliṉ iḷiyeṉṉum
   narampu, paṭṭaṭai yeṉappaṭṭatu. yāḻiṉ mēṟpaṇkaḷai iḷi muṟaiyālē vaittu
   eṉka. iḷi muṟaiyāvatu caṭcak kiramam. āṅku; acai.
   65. oṟṟi-niṉaintu.
   67. vāra nilattaik kēṭiṉṟu pārttu eṉpatu-mutaṉaṭai, vāram, kūṭai, tiraḷ
   eṉṟu collappaṭṭa iyakkam nāṉkiṉum mutal naṭai mikavum tāḻnta celaviṉai
   yuṭaittākalāṉum, tiraḷ mika muṭukiya naṭaiyiṉai yuṭaittākalāṉum ivai
   tavirntu, iṭaippaṭṭa vārap pāṭal colloḻukkamum icaiyoḻukkamum
   uṭaittākalāṉum, kūṭaippāṭal coṟceṟivum, icaic ceṟivum uṭaittākalāṉum
   ciṟappunōkki, avviraṇṭiṉuḷḷum vārappāṭalai aḷavu nirampa
   niṟuttavallaṉāy eṉka. eṉavē, kūṭaippāṭalum amaivatāyiṟṟu.
   69. icaikkum eṉpatai eḻutteḻuttāka icaikkum eṉṟum collappaṭṭa
   yāvaṟṟaiyum vaḻuviṉṟicaikkum eṉṟum pirittuk kūṭṭuka.
   aṟintu mayalaṟappeytu uṇarviṉaṉāki aṟintu porunti vaittu vaḷarttu oṟṟi
   iyakki vaittup pārttu icaikkuṅ kuḻalōṉ eṉka.
   (yāḻāciriyaṉ amaiti)
   ippakutiyiṉ urai naṉku viḷaṅkutaṟporuṭṭu icaiyaip paṟṟiya cila
   koḷkaikaḷai mutaṟkaṇ viḷakkutum:-
   tamiḻil icai eṉpatu curam irākam eṉṉum iru poruḷilum vaḻaṅkum.
   irākattiṟku icaiyeṉṉum peyaraṉṟip paṇ eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭu. vaṭamoḻiyil
   curutiyeṉṟu collappaṭuvatu tamiḻil alaku eṉṟum, māttirai yeṉṟum
   vaḻaṅkum. maṟṟum, curam eṉpataṟku narampu eṉṟa peyarum, paṇ eṉpataṟku
   yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum. ēḻicaikaṭkum tamiḻilē kural tuttam,
   kaikkiḷai, uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉpaṉa peyarkaḷām. ēḻucuraṅkaṭkum
   vaṭamoḻiyilē caṭcam, riṭapam, kāntāram, mattimam, pañcamam, taivatam,
   niṭātam eṉpaṉa peyarkaḷām.
   tamiḻil ēḻicaikaṭkum irupattiraṇṭu alakukaḷ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa; avai kural
   mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṟaiyē 4,4,2,4,3,2 ākum. itaṉai,
   "kuraltuttam nāṉku kiḷaimūṉ ṟiraṇṭām
       kuraiyā uḻaiyiḷi nāṉku- viraiyā
       viḷariyeṉiṉ mūṉṟiraṇṭu tārameṉac coṉṉār
       kaḷaricēr kaṇṇuṟ ṟavar."

   eṉpataṉā ṉaṟika.
   vaṭamoḻiyilum ēḻu curaṅkaṭkum irupattiraṇṭu curutikaḷē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
   avai caṭcam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku muṟaiyē 4,3,2,4,4,3,2 ākum.
   inta icai allatu curaṅkaḷiṉ varicaikkut tamiḻil kōvaiyeṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
   ivvaricai yamaippukkaḷ,āyappālai,caturappālai,vaṭṭappālai,
   tirikōṇappālai,eṉa,nālvakaippaṭukiṉṟaṉa;ivaṟṟiṉiṉṟum uṇṭāvatu irākam
   allatu paṇ ākum.
   ēḻicai yeṉappaṭum curaṅkaḷ ēḻiṉaiyum māṟun tiṟattiṉālēyē palavakaip
   paṇkaḷ allatu irākaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa; ākaliṉ curaṅkaḷaippaṟṟiya
   ceytikaḷai mutalil naṉku terintu koḷḷutal vēṇṭum. kural mutalāya
   ēḻaṉuḷ, tārattil uḻaiyum, uḻaiyiṟ kuralum, kuralil iḷiyum, iḷiyil
   tuttamum, tuttattil viḷariyum, viḷariyiṟ kaikkiḷaiyum piṟakkum; ivaṟṟuḷ
   mutaliṟ ṟōṉṟiyatu tāram.
   "tārattuṭ ṭōṉṟum uḻaiyuḻai yuṭṭōṉṟum
       oruṅ kural kurali ṉuṭṭōṉṟic – cērumiḷi
       yuṭṭōṉṟun tuttattuṭ ṭōṉṟum viḷariyuṭ
       kaikkiḷai tōṉṟum piṟappu".

   eṉpatu kāṇka. tārattuḷ ataṟ kaintāvatākiya uḻaiyum, uḻaiyuḷ ataṟ
   kaintāvatākiya kuralum, immuṟaiyē ēṉaiyavum tōṉṟiṉa veṉpatu
   aṟiyaṟpālatu. ivvēḻicaikaḷum vaṭṭappālai muṟaiyil ōr irāci maṇṭalattiṉ
   paṉṉiraṇṭu irukkaikaḷaiyum iṭamākak koḷḷum eṉpatu piṉṉar āycciyar
   kuravaiyuḷ viḷakkappaṭum.
   ōr irākam allatu paṇṇiṉai uṇṭākkutaṟ poruṭṭu mutalilē toṭaṅkappeṟum
   curam kural eṉpatākum. ēḻu curaṅkaḷil etaṉaiyum kuralāka niṟuttutal
   uṇṭu; aḵtāvatu kuralē kuralākavum, tuttam kuralākavum, kaikkiḷai
   kuralākavum iṅṅaṉam toṭakkappeṟum eṉpatām.
   curaṅkaḷaikkoṇṭu icaiyai eḻupputaṟkuk karuviyākak koḷḷappaṭṭa
   eḻuttukkaḷ carikamapatani eṉṉum ēḻumām. ivveḻuttukkaḷ caṭcam mutaliya
   peyarkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷ eṉṟu koḷvaṉa poruntāmaiyāl, ivai kuṟiyīṭāka
   amaittukkoḷḷap peṟṟaṉavātal vēṇṭum. vaṭanūṟṟuṟaipōya cilarum iṅṅaṉam
   karutuvar.
   " ā ī ū ē ai ō au veṉum
       ivvē ḻeḻuttum ēḻicaik kuriya"

   eṉat tivākaram kūṟutaliṉ, ā mutaliya neṭṭuyir ēḻaṉaiyum karuviyākak
   koṇṭu paṇṭait tamiḻmakkaḷ icaipāṭiṉarātalum vēṇṭum.
   tamiḻilē irākaṅkaḷ paṇ eṉavum, tiṟam eṉavum iruvakaip paṭum.
   "nirainaram piṟṟē paṇṇeṉa lākum", "kuṟainaram piṟṟē tiṟameṉap paṭumē"
   eṉṉum tivākara nūṟpākkaḷāl ēḻu narampāṉum iyaṉṟatu paṇṇām eṉpatum, āṟu
   aintu nāṉku eṉak kuṟainta narampukaḷāṉ iyaṉṟaṉa tiṟamām eṉpatum
   peṟappaṭum. tiṟattai mūṉṟu vakaippaṭuttup paṇṇiyaṟṟiṟam, tiṟam,
   tiṟattiṟam eṉa vaḻaṅkutalumuṇṭu. paṇṇum, tiṟamūṉṟum ākiya innālvakai
   irākaṅkaḷaiyum kuṟikkum vaṭamoḻippeyarkaḷ campūrṇam, cāṭavam, auṭavam,
   caturttam eṉpaṉa. innāṉkum tamiḻil paṇ, tiṟam eṉa iraṇṭākak
   kūṟappaṭutalōṭu yāvum paṇṇeṉṟē kūṟappaṭutalum uṇṭu.
   tamiḻil aintiṇaikkumuriya paṇkaḷ kuṟiñciyāḻ, pālaiyāḻ, mullaiyāḻ,
   neytalyāḻ, marutayāḻ eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ neytal yāḻukku viḷari eṉpatum
   peyar. itu tiṟaṉil yāḻ eṉappaṭutaliṉ, tiṟaṅkaḷuṭaiya ēṉai nāṉkumē
   perumpaṇkaḷ eṉappaṭum; "yāma yāḻppeyar kuṟiñci yāḻum, cevvaḻi yāḻppeyar
   mullai yāḻum, pālai yāḻum, maruta yāḻumeṉa, nālvakai yāḻum nāṟperum
   paṇṇē" eṉpatu tivākaram. itiliruntu kuṟiñci yāḻukku yāma yāḻ eṉṟa
   peyarum, mullai yāḻukkuc cevvaḻi yāḻ eṉṟa peyarum uṇṭeṉpatu pulaṉām.
   innūṟ perum paṇṇum piṟakkumāṟu "tārat tuḻaitōṉṟap pālaiyāḻ taṇkural,
   orumuḻai tōṉṟak kuṟiñciyāḻ-nērē, iḷikuraliṟ ṟōṉṟa marutayāḻ tuttam,
   iḷiyiṟ piṟakkaneyta liyāḻ" eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. itil neytal yāḻ eṉṟatu
   cevvaḻi yātal vēṇṭum. eṉavē tāram kuralāka uḻai ataṟkuk kiḷaiyākat
   tōṉṟavatu kuṟiñciyāḻ eṉavum, kural kuralāka iḷi ataṟkuk kiḷaiyākat
   tōṉṟuvatu cevvaḻi yāḻ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum eṉka. kiḷaiyāvatu niṉṟa
   narampiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟum narampu; niṉṟa narampiṟku aintāvatu narampu.
   ivvāṟē niṉṟa narampiṟku nāṉkāvatu naṭpunarampeṉṟum, mūṉṟāvatum āṟāvatum
   pakai narampeṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatum ēḻāvatum iṇai narampeṉṟum aṟika.
   iṉi, muṟkuṟitta nāṉku paṇkaḷuḷ pālaiyāḻiliruntu cempālai,
   paṭumalaippālai, cevvaḻippālai, arumpālai, kōṭippālai, viḷarippālai,
   mēṟcempālai eṉṉum ēḻupālai yicaikaḷ piṟakkum; ivai piṟakkumāṟu mēl
   ikkātaiyuḷḷum, āycciyar kuravaiyuḷḷum viḷakkamām.
   iṉi, pālai, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi yeṉṉum nāṟperum paṇkaḷil ovvoṉṟum
   akanilai, puṟanilai, arukiyal, perukiyal eṉṉum iṉa vēṟupāṭṭāl nannāṉku
   ākap perumpaṇkaḷ patiṉāṟākum. nāṟperum paṇkaḷuḷḷē pālai yāḻukku aintum,
   kuṟiñci yāḻukku eṭṭum, maruta yāḻukku nāṉkum, cevvaḻi yāvukku nāṉkum
   āka irupattoru tiṟaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. "īriru paṇṇum eḻumūṉṟu tiṟaṉum" eṉpatu
   piṅkalam. tiṟam irupattoṉṟu akanilai, puṟanilai, arukiyal, perukiyal
   eṉṉum vēṟupāṭṭāl eṇpattu nāṉku ākum. eṉavē, paṇ patiṉāṟum, tiṟam
   eṇpattu nāṉkum cērntu nūṟu eṉṉum tokaiyiṉavākiṉṟaṉa. piṅkala nikaṇṭilē
   tiṟaṅkaḷiṉ vakai yāvaṟṟiṟkum peyar kūṟi muṭintapiṉ, "tārap paṇṭiṟam
   paiyuḷ kāñci paṭumalai yivai nūṟṟu mūṉṟu tiṟattaṉa" eṉṟu
   kūṟappaṭṭiruttaliṉ, tārap paṇṭiṟam, paiyuḷ kāñci, paṭumalai eṉṉum mūṉṟu
   tiṟaṅkaḷum muṟkūṟiya nūṟṟuṭaṉ cērappaṇkaḷ nūṟṟu mūṉṟu eṉṉum tokaipeṟum.
   paṇ nūṟṟumūṉṟeṉṟal peru vaḻakku. perum paṇkaḷiṉ vakai patiṉāṟaṉuṭ
   paṉṉiraṇṭukkum, tiṟaṅkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉ vakaiyumākiya eṇpattu nāṉkiṟkum
   peyar piṅkala nikaṇṭiṟ kāṇka.
   iṉi, tamiḻilē kural mutalākavum, vaṭamoḻiyilē caṭcam mutalākavum ēḻu
   curaṅkaḷum peyar kūṟappaṭutaliṉ, kuralum caṭcamum oṉṟeṉavum, tuttamum
   riṭapamum oṉṟeṉavum, ivvāṟē ēṉaiyavum muṟaiyē ovvoṉṟā meṉavum karututal
   kūṭum. āyiṉ ivaṟṟiṟkuk kūṟappaṭum alakum oḷiyum piṟappiṭamum
   vēṟupaṭutaliṉ ammuṟaiyē ivviraṇṭum oṉṟeṉal amaiyāmai peṟṟām. īṇṭu
   alakiṉai nōkkutum:-
   kural-4, tuttam-4, kaikkiḷai-3, uḻai-2, iḷi-4, viḷari-3, tāram-2.
   caṭcam-4, riṭapam-3, kāntāram-2, mattimam-4, pañcamam-4, taivatam-3,
   niṭātam-2.
   ivaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭāvatum mūṉṟāvatum nāṉkāvatum māttiraiyil ovvāmai kāṇka.
   caṭcam mutaliyavaṟṟiṟku iṅkē kāṭṭiya curutikaḷiṉ aḷavē cāraṅkatēvar
   iyaṟṟiya caṅkītaratṉākaram mutaliya nūṟkaḷ palavaṟṟilum kāṇappaṭutaliṉ,
   ammuṟaiyē tamiḻukkum poruttamātal vēṇṭum. kural eṉpataṉaic caṭcam eṉṟu
   koḷḷātu mattimam eṉak koḷḷiṉ iraṇṭilum curutiyaḷavukaḷ ottu
   viṭukiṉṟaṉa.
   kural-4, tuttam-4, kaikkiḷai-3, uḻai-2, iḷi-4, viḷari-3, tāram-2.
   ma-4, pa-4, ta-3, ni-2, ca-4, ri-3, ka-2
   curukkaṅkaruti mattimam mutaliya peyarkaḷ ma mutaliya eḻuttukkaḷāṟ
   kuṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. mēlē kāṭṭiyavaṟṟil iru tiṟattum alaku ottiruttal
   kāṇka. ivvāṟṟāl paṇṭait tamiḻ icai nūlōr vaṭanūṉ muṟaiyilamainta
   mattimattai ārampa curamākak koṇṭaṉa reṉpatu pōtarum. eṉavē, tārattu
   uḻai tōṉṟum eṉṟa muṟaippaṭi kāntārattil niṭātamum, niṭātattil
   mattimamum, mattimattil caṭcamum, caṭcattil pañcamum, pañcamattil
   riṭapamum, riṭapattil taivatamum piṟakkum eṉak koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟai
   naṉku niṉaivil vaittukkoḷḷiṉ, piṉ kūṟappaṭum icaiyiyalpukaḷ perumpālum
   mayakkamaṟa viḷaṅkum. mika nuṭpamāṉa icai yiyalpukaḷai aṟiya viḻaivōr
   icainūṟkaḷaik kaṟṟum icaip payiṟci ceytum aṟital vēṇṭum.
   -----------------
   70. īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟaik kēḷviyiṉ

   īrēḻ toṭutta cemmuṟaik kēḷviyiṉ-cevviya muṟaiyē iraṇṭēḻākat
   toṭukkappaṭṭa āyap pālaiyāy niṉṟa patiṉāṟ kōvaiyil,
   ---------
   71. ōrēḻ pālai niṟuttal vēṇṭi

   ōrēḻ pālai niṟuttal vēṇṭi-cempālai paṭumalaip pālai cevvaḻippālai
   arumpālai kōṭippālai viḷarippālai mēṟcempālai yeṉappaṭṭa ēḻu
   pālaiyiṉaiyum iṇai narampu toṭuttu nirampa niṟuttik kāṭṭal kāraṇamāka,
   ----------------
   72. vaṉmaiyiṟ kiṭanta tāra pākamum

   vaṉmaiyiṟ kiṭanta tārapākamum-ippālaiyiṉ muṭivuttāṉamāy valinta
   nilaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tārampeṟṟa iraṇṭalakil ōralakaiyum,
   --------------------
   73. meṉmaiyiṟ kiṭanta kuraliṉ pākamum

   meṉmaiyiṟ kiṭanta kuraliṉ pākamum-mutaṟṟāṉamāy meliviṉiṟkum kural
   narampu peṟṟa nālalakil iraṇṭalakaiyum,
   -----------------
   74-5. meykkiḷai narampiṟ kaikkiḷai koḷḷak
       kaikkiḷai

   meykkiḷai narampiṟ kaikkiḷai koḷḷak kaikkiḷai-tāranarampil antarak
   kōlilē kaikkiḷaiyāka niṟuttat tārantāṉ kaikkiḷaiyāyiṟṟu;
   ---------------
   75-7. oḻinta pākamum poṟpuṭait
       taḷarāt tāram viḷarikku īttuk
       kiḷaivaḻip paṭṭaṉaḷ

   oḻintapākamum poṟpuṭait taḷarāttāram viḷarikku īttuk kiḷaivaḻip
   paṭṭaṉaḷ-taḷarāta aḻakuṭaiya tāra narampil oḻinta ōralakaiyum
   viḷarikkuttara avviḷari tutta narampāyiṟṟu;
   ----------------
   77-8. āṅkē kiḷaiyum
       taṉkiḷai aḻivukaṇṭu avaḷvayiṟ cēra

   āṅkē kiḷaiyum taṉ kiḷai aḻivu kaṇṭu avaḷ vayiṟ cēra-ammuṟaiyē iḷiyum
   taṉ kiḷaiyākiya kuraliṉ aḻiviṉaikkaṇṭu ataṉ pāṟ cēravum,
   ----------------
   79. ēṉai makaḷirum kiḷaivaḻic cēra

   ēṉai makaḷirum kiḷaivaḻic cēra-ēṉaiya uḻai mutalāyiṉavum tattamakkuk
   kiḷaiyāyiṉavaṟṟiṟ cēravum ivvāṟāya patiṉāṟ kōvaiyilē,
   ----------------
   80-1. mēlatu uḻaiyiḷi kīḻatu kaikkiḷai
       vampuuṟu marapiṉ cempālai āyatu

   mēlatu uḻaiyiḷi kīḻatu kaikkiḷai vampuṟu marapiṟ cempālai
   āyatu-uḻaimutalākak kaikkiḷaiyiṟutiyāka melivu nāṉkum camam ēḻum valivu
   mūṉṟumāyp putumaiyuṟṟa muṟaiyālē uḻai kuralākac cempālaiyāyatu;
   ---------------
   82-3. iṟuti āti āka āṅkuavai
       peṟumuṟai vanta peṟṟiyiṉ nīṅkātu

   iṟuti ātiyāka āṅkavai peṟumuṟai vanta peṟṟiyiṉ nīṅkātu-iṟutiyāy niṉṟa
   kaikkiḷai mutalākavuḷḷa kaikkiḷai tuttam kural eṉṉumavai tām tōṉṟiya
   iṭamuṟaiyāṉa iyalpiṉ nīṅkātu,
   ----------------
   84-5. paṭumalai cevvaḻi pakararum pālaieṉak
       kuralkural ākat taṟkiḻamai tirintapiṉ

   paṭumalai cevvaḻi arumpālai eṉak kural kuralākattaṟkiḻamai
   tirintapiṉ-kaikkiḷai kuralākap paṭumalaip pālaiyum tuttam kuralākac
   cevvaḻip pālaiyum kural kuralāka arumpālaiyum eṉa muṟaiyē tirintapiṉ,
   ----------
   86. muṉṉataṉ vakaiyē muṟaimaiyiṉ tirintuāṅku

   muṉṉataṉ vakaiyē muṟaimaiyil tirintu āṅku-muṉpiṟpaṭiyē muṟaimaiyiṉ
   vēṟupaṭṭu,
   ------------
   87. iḷimuta lākiya ērpaṭu kiḻamaiyum

   iḷi mutalākiya etirpaṭu kiḻamaiyum- tāram kuralākak kōṭippālaiyum
   viḷarikuralāka viḷarippālaiyum iḷikuralāka mēṟ cempālai eṉat tiriya,
   --------------
   88. kōṭi viḷari mēṟcem pālaieṉa

   kōṭi viḷari mēṟcempālai eṉa-tāram kuralākak kōṭip pālaiyum
   viḷarikuralāka viḷarippālaiyum iḷikuralāka mēṟcempālai eṉat tiriya,
   ---------------
   89. nīṭik kiṭanta kēḷvik kiṭakkaiyiṉ

   nīṭikkiṭanta kēḷvik kiṭakkaiyiṉ-neṭitāyk kiṭanta curaṅkaḷiṉ iṭattē,
   -----------
   90. iṇainarampu uṭaiyaṉa aṇaivuṟak koṇṭuāṅku

   iṇai narampuṭaiyaṉa aṇaivuṟakkoṇṭu āṅku-mutalum iṟutiyumākavuḷḷa
   narampukaḷaip poruntak koṇṭu,
   -----------
   91. yāḻmēṟ pālai iṭamuṟai meliyak

   yāḻ mēṟpālai iṭamuṟai meliya-yāḻiṉiṭattu arumpālai mutalāyiṉa iṭamuṟai
   meliyavum,
   --------------
   92. kuḻalmēṟ kōṭi valamuṟai meliya

   kuḻalmēṟkōṭi valamuṟai meliya-kuḻaliṉiṭattuk kōṭippālai mutalāyiṉa
   valamuṟai meliyavum,
   --------------
   93 – 4. valivum melivum camaṉum ellām
       poliyak kōtta pulamai yōṉuṭaṉ,

   valivum melivum camaṉum ellām poliyak kōtta pulamaiyōṉuṭaṉ-valivum
   melivum camaṉum viḷaṅkavum narappaṭaivu keṭāmalum paṇṇīramai mutalāyiṉa
   kuṉṟāmalum eḻuttukkaḷāl icai ceyya valla yāḻiciriyaṉum;
   -----------
   70. kēḷvi-narampu; curam kural mutalāya ēḻu narampiṉaiyum iraṭṭitta
   patiṉāṟkōvai yeṉka.
   ------------
   74. tārattiṟkuk kaikkiḷai iṭamuṟaiyāl aintāvatākaliṉ kiḷai narampeṉṟār.
   kiḷai-aintāvatu narampu.
   --------------
   77. viḷarikkut tuttam ammuṟaiyē aintāvatākaliṉ kiḷai vaḻippaṭṭaṉaḷ
   eṉṟār. kiḷaiyum-iḷiyum; kuralukku iḷi aintāvatākaliṉ ataṉaik kiḷai
   eṉṉum peyarāṟ kūṟiṉār; aṉṟi, iḷiyeṉpatē eḻutuvōrāṟ kiḷaiyeṉat tiripuṟṟa
   teṉalumām. arumpatavuraiyāciriyar coṟkaḷai eñcāteṭuttup poruḷ
   kūṟimuṭikkuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭaiyarallar; maṟṟu aṭiyārkku nallārē aṅṅaṉam
   uraikkuṅ kaṭappāṭṭiṉar. āyiṉ avar ekkāraṇattāṉō icaippakutiyil
   muṉṉavaruraittavaṟṟaiyē tāmum uraittu, mūlattiluḷḷa coṟkaḷ
   palavaṟṟiṟkup poruḷum muṭipum terikkātu pōyiṉar. ataṉāl iññāṉṟu avaṟṟiṉ
   poruḷ aṟital aritāyiṉamaiyiṉ eḻutuvōrālum, patippippōrālum vaḻukkaḷ
   nikaḻtal iyalpēyākum.
   ‘arumpatavuraiyilum,aṭiyārkkunallāruraiyilum muṟaiyē ‘ikkiramattiṉālē,’
   ‘iḷikkiramattālē’ (cilap araṅ. aṭi.63-4) eṉavum patiṉāṟkōvai polintu,’
   ‘patiṉāṟkōvai kōliṉitu’ (aṭi.70) eṉavum, ‘varappaṭṭa pālai,’
   ‘vaṭṭappālai’ (aṭi. 72-3) eṉavum, iṅṅaṉam eṇṇiṟantaṉa tirintu, tiruttam
   peṟāmalē muntiya patippukkaḷ palavaṟṟilum kāṇappaṭutalai nōkkiṉ
   mūlapāṭam uruccitaintu vaḻuppaṭṭiruttal aritaṉ ṟeṉpatu pulaṉām.
   ------------------
   77-9. narampukaḷai makaḷirāka uruvakappaṭutti, ‘vaḻippaṭṭaṉaḷ’ eṉavum,
   ‘avaḷ vayiṉ’ eṉavum, ‘ēṉai makaḷirum’ eṉavum kūṟiṉār. itaṉai
   aṟiyamāṭṭātu, uraiyilē ‘peṇṭirkkuriya tāṉamuṭaiya’ eṉa vantiruppatu
   koṇṭu, itu peṇṭirkaḷ iṉitu pāṭutaṟkēṟṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiṉārumuḷar.
   kiḷaivaḻiccēra nīṭikkiṭanta kēḷvikkiṭakkaiyiṉ iṇai narampuṭaiyaṉa
   aṇaivuṟak koṇṭu eṉa iyaikka.
   cemmuṟaik kēḷviyākum patiṉāṟkōvai varumāṟu :
       (1) kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai, uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram;
       (2) kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai, uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram .
       itiṉiṉṟum uṇṭāya kēḷvik kiṭakkaiyākum patiṉāṟ kōvai varumāṟu :
       (1) uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram, kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai;
       (2) uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram, kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai.
       ivai muṟaiyē,
       ma pa ta ni ca ri ka – ma pa ta ni ca ri ka eṉavum,
       ni ca ri ka ma pa ta – ni ca ri ka ma pa ta eṉavum kūṟik koḷka.

   ---------------
   80. cemmuṟai māṟivanta patiṉāṟ kōvaiyil uḻai mutalum kaikkiḷai
   iṟutiyumāy niṟṟal kāṇka. ‘uḻaiyiḷi’ eṉṉum pāṭam vaḻuppaṭṭatākal vēṇṭum;
   īṇṭu iḷi eṉpataṟkup poruḷoṉṟu miṉmaiyum, paḻaiya uraikaḷil iccol vaṟitē
   viṭappaṭṭiruttaluṅ kāṇka.
   -----------
   82. iṟuti ātiyāka-iṟutiyākavuḷḷa kaikkiḷai mutalāka. kaikkiḷai iṟutiyil
   niṟṟal kāṇka.
   -------------
   85. kural kuralāka eṉpatu oṉṟoṉṟāka eṉpatupōl niṉṟa aṭukku.
   taṟkiḻamai-muṟai niraṉiṟai.
   --------------
   87. etirpaṭukiḻamai-etir niraṉiṟai.
   -------------
   90. iṇainarampu-mutalum iṟutiyumākavuḷḷa narampu; uḻai mutaṟ kaikkiḷai
   yiṟutiyum, kaikkiḷai mutal tuttam iṟutiyum, ivvāṟē ēṉaiyavum mutalum
   iṟutiyumāki yeṉka; iraṭṭitta narampu eṉṟalumām. uḻai, kaikkiḷai,
   tuttam, kural, tāram, viḷari, iḷiyeṉa iṭa muṟaiyāl ovvoṉṟum kuralākac
   cempālai mutalāyiṉa tōṉṟiṉamai aṟiyaṟpāṟṟu. ivvēḻu paṇkaḷum piṟa
   muṟaiyāl uṇṭāvaṉa vēṉiṟ kātaiyilum āycciyar kuravaiyilum aṟiyappaṭum.
   -------------
   9ka-u. kuḻalmēl kōṭi eṉṟataṉāl yāḻmēl arumpālaiyeṉa varuvikkappaṭṭatu.
   melital-iṟaṅkutal; avarōkaṇam.
   --------------
   93. valivu-mēl; uccam; tāram. melivu-kīḻ; mantam. camam-mattimam. ivai
   ōcaiyiṉ mūvakai iyakkam.
   āṅkavai, tirintāṅku. koṇṭāṅku eṉpavaṟṟil āṅku acai.
   niṟuttal vēṇṭip poliyak kōtta pulamaiyōṉ eṉa muṭikka.
   ------------
   (araṅkiṉ amaiti)
   95-113. eṇṇiya nūlōr iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻāatu
       maṇṇakam oruvaḻi vakuttaṉar koṇṭu
       puṇṇiya neṭuvaraip pōkiya neṭuṅkaḻaik
       kaṇṇiṭai orucāṇ vaḷarntatu koṇṭu
       nūlneṟi marapiṉ araṅkam aḷakkum
       kōlaḷavu irupattu nālviral āka
       eḻukōl akalattu eṇkōl nīḷattu
       orukōl uyarattu uṟuppiṉatu āki
       uttarap palakaiyoṭu araṅkiṉ palakai
       vaitta iṭainilam nāṟkōl āka
       ēṟṟa vāyil iraṇṭuṭaṉ poliyat
       tōṟṟiya araṅkil toḻutaṉar ēttap
       pūtarai eḻuti mēlnilai vaittut
       tūṇniḻal puṟappaṭa māṇviḷakku eṭuttuāṅku
       orumuka eḻiṉiyum porumuka eḻiṉiyum
       karantuvaral eḻiṉiyum purintuṭaṉ vakuttuāṅku
       ōviya vitāṉattu uraipeṟu nittilattu
       mālait tāmam vaḷaiyuṭaṉ nāṟṟi
       viruntupaṭak kiṭanta aruntoḻil araṅkattup

   95 - eṇṇiya nūlōr iyalpiṉiṉ vaḻātu maṇṇakam oruvaḻi vakuttaṉar
   koṇṭu-eṇṇappaṭṭa ciṟpa nūlāciriyar vakutta iyalpukaḷiṉ vaḻuvāta vakai
   araṅkiyaṟṟutaṟkuk kuṟṟam nīṅkiṉa ōriṭattil nilam vakuttuk koṇṭu,
   puṇṇiya neṭuvaraip pōkiya neṭuṅkaḻai kaṇṇiṭai orucāṇ vaḷarntatu
   koṇṭu-potiyiṉ mutalāya tippiya malaippakkaṅkaḷilē nīṇṭu vaḷarnta
   mūṅkiliṟ kaṇṇōṭu kaṇṇiṭai oru cāṇāka vaḷarntatu koṇṭu, nūlneṟi
   marapiṉ-nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭum muṟaiyālē, araṅkam aḷakkum kōl aḷavu
   irupattu nālviral āka-araṅkam iyaṟṟutaṟku aḷakkuṅkōl uttamaṉ kaip
   peruviral irupattunāṉku koṇṭa aḷaviṉatāka naṟukki, eḻukōl akalattu
   eṇkōl nīḷattu orukōl uyarattu uṟuppiṉatāki-akkōlāl eḻukōl akalamum
   eṇkōl nīḷamum orukōṟ kuṟaṭṭuyaramum uṭaiyatāy, uttarappalakaiyōṭu
   araṅkiṉpalakai vaitta iṭainilam nāṟkōl āka-tūṇiṉmītuvaitta uttarap
   palakaikkum araṅkiṉ taḷattiṟkiṭṭa palakaikkum iṭainiṉṟa nilam nāṉku
   kōlaḷaviṉatākavum, ēṟṟa vāyil iraṇṭu uṭaṉ poliya-avvaḷavukaṭkup porunta
   vakukkappaṭṭa vāyil iraṇṭu viḷaṅkavum, tōṟṟiya araṅkil-ceyyappaṭṭa
   araṅkilē, toḻutaṉar ēttap pūtarai eḻuti mēl nilaivaittu-nālvakai
   varuṇap pūtaraiyum eḻuti yāvarum pukaḻntu vaṇaṅka mēṉilattē vaittu,
   tūṇniḻaṟ puṟappaṭa māṇviḷakku eṭuttu āṅku – tūṇkaḷiṉ niḻalnāyakap
   pattiyiṉ kaṇṇum avaiyiṉ kaṇṇum paṭātapaṭi māṭcimaippaṭṭa nilaiviḷakku
   niṟutti, orumuka eḻiṉiyum porumuka eḻiṉiyum karantu varal eḻiṉiyum
   purintu uṭaṉvakuttu āṅku-iṭattūṇ nilaiyiṭattē uruvu tiraiyāka orumuka
   veḻiṉiyum iraṇṭu valattūṇiṭattum uruvu tiraiyākap porumuka veḻiṉiyum
   mēṟkaṭṭut tiraiyākak karantuvara leḻiṉiyum toḻiṟpāṭṭuṭaṉē vakuttu,
   ōviya vitāṉattu-cittira vitāṉattaiyum amaittu, uraipeṟu nittilattu
   mālait tāmam vaḷaivuṭaṉ nāṟṟi-pukaḻamainta muttumālaikaḷāṟ cariyum
   tūkkum tāmamumākat toṅkaviṭṭu, viruntupaṭakkiṭanta aruntoḻil
   araṅkattu-putumaiyuṭaittākap poruntiya ariya toḻilaiyuṭaiya araṅkiṉkaṇ;
   95-6, nūlōr-nāṭaka nūlōr eṉpar arumpatavurai yāciriyar. ivvulakil
   nūlvaḻi araṅku ceyyumiṭattut teyvattāṉamum paḷḷiyum antaṇarirukkaiyum
   kiṇaṟum kuḷaṉum kā×m mutalāyiṉa aḻiyāta iyalpiṉaiyuṭaittāy,
   niṟukkappaṭṭa kuḻippuḻuti kuḻikkottu, kallappaṭṭa maṇ iṉiya nāṟṟamum
   iṉiya cuvaiyum uṭaiyatāy, tāṉum tiṇṇitāy, eṉpum umiyum paraluñ cērnta
   nilam, kaḷittarai, uvarttarai, īḷaittarai, pollāc cāmpaṟṟarai,
   poṭittarai eṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa oḻintu, ūriṉ naṭuvaṇatākiyuḷḷa nilattilē
   tērōṭum vītikaḷ etirmukamākak koḷḷappaṭum eṉpar.
   nilam vaṉpāl, meṉpāl, iṭaippāleṉṟu mūvakaippaṭum; avaṟṟuḷ vaṉpālāvatu
   tōṇṭappaṭṭa kuḻiyil ammaṇṇiṉaip peyyiṉ kuḻiyiṉmaṇ mikuvatu; meṉpālāvatu
   kuṟaivatu; iṭaippālāvatu ottiruppatu. iṭaippālē vēṇṭappaṭuvatām. maṇṇiṉ
   cuvaikaḷuḷ tuvarppu accamum, puḷippu nōyum, kāḻppup paci nīṭutalum,
   kaippukkēṭum, uvarppuk kalakkamum, tittippu aṉpum viḷaikkumākaliṉ
   tittippē koḷḷattakuvatām.
   97. pōkiya-nīṇṭa.
   100. uttamarāvār; mikka neṭumaiyum mikka kuṟumaiyumillōr. aṇu

   nellu eṭṭukkoṇṭatu peruviralām.
   105. vāyil iraṇṭāvaṉa: uṭpukavum puṟañcellavum camaittavai. maṟṟum,
   karantu pōkkiṭaṉum, kūttar kuṭiñaippaḷḷiyum, araṅkamum, ataṉetir maṉṉar
   māntarōṭirukkum avaiyaraṅkamum, puviniṟai māntar poruntiya kōṭṭiyum
   mutalāyiṉa koḷḷappaṭum.
   107. pūtar-varuṇap pūtar;ivar vacciratēkaṉ, vacciratantaṉ, varuṇaṉ,

   mutaliyaṉa viḷaṅka iṅṅaṉam varuṇappūtam nāṉkum vakukkappaṭṭaṉavākum.
   ivaṟṟiṉiyalpu piṉṉar aḻaṟpaṭu kātaiyuḷ viritturaikkappaṭum.
   111. urai-pukaḻ. mēl ūrkāṇ kātaiyil "cantira kuruvē yaṅkā rakaṉeṉa,

   eṉka.
   vakuttaṉar koṇṭuvaittu eṭuttu vakuttu nāṟṟikkiṭanta araṅkatteṉka.
